   52 | function assertFunction(api, name) {
   53 |   if (!api || typeof api[name] !== "function") {
   54 |     throw new Error(
   55 |       `Função pública TOP3 indisponível: ${name}`
   56 |     );
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   return api[name];
   60 | }
   61 | 
   62 | function getTop3Capabilities() {
   63 |   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
   64 | 
   65 |   return Object.keys(api)
   66 |     .filter((name) => typeof api[name] === "function")
   67 |     .sort();
   68 | }
   69 | 
   70 | function computeConditionalNextTop3(input = {}) {
   71 |   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
   72 | 
   73 |   return assertFunction(
   74 |     api,
   75 |     "computeConditionalNextTop3"
   76 |   )(input);
   77 | }
   78 | 
   79 | function computeConditionalNextTop3V2(input = {}) {
   80 |   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
   81 | 
   82 |   return assertFunction(
   83 |     api,
   84 |     "computeConditionalNextTop3V2"
   85 |   )(input);
   86 | }
   87 | 
   88 | function computeStatisticalTop3V3(input = {}) {
   89 |   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
   90 | 
   91 |   return assertFunction(
   92 |     api,
   93 |     "computeStatisticalTop3V3"
   94 |   )(input);
   95 | }

LINHAS: 21-86
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   21 |   "18": "Porco",
   22 |   "19": "Pavão",
   23 |   "20": "Peru",
   24 |   "21": "Touro",
   25 |   "22": "Tigre",
   26 |   "23": "Urso",
   27 |   "24": "Veado",
   28 |   "25": "Vaca",
   29 | };
   30 | 
   31 | function pad2(n) {
   32 |   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   33 | }
   34 | 
   35 | function toNum(v) {
   36 |   const n = Number(v);
   37 |   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
   38 | }
   39 | 
   40 | function rankingToPredictions(ranking = [], opts = {}) {
   41 |   const limit = Math.max(1, Number(opts.limit || 10));
   42 | 
   43 |   const rows = (Array.isArray(ranking) ? ranking : [])
   44 |     .map((item) => {
   45 |       const grupo = pad2(item.grupo || item.grupoNum || item.group || item.id);
   46 |       const aparicoes = toNum(item.total ?? item.apar ?? item.count ?? 0);
   47 | 
   48 |       return {
   49 |         grupo,
   50 |         animal: item.animal || item.nome || ANIMALS[grupo] || null,
   51 |         aparicoes,
   52 |         raw: item,
   53 |       };
   54 |     })
   55 |     .filter((x) => /^\d{2}$/.test(x.grupo))
   56 |     .sort((a, b) => b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes || Number(a.grupo) - Number(b.grupo))
   57 |     .slice(0, limit);
   58 | 
   59 |   const max = rows.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, x.aparicoes), 0) || 1;
   60 | 
   61 |   return rows.map((x, idx) => {
   62 |     const score = Math.round((x.aparicoes / max) * 100);
   63 |     const confidence = Math.max(1, Math.round(score - idx * 2));
   64 | 
   65 |     return {
   66 |       type: "grupo",
   67 |       grupo: x.grupo,
   68 |       animal: x.animal,
   69 |       score,
   70 |       confidence,
   71 |       reasons: [
   72 |         `Ranking V1: ${x.aparicoes} aparições no recorte analisado`,
   73 |         `Posição ${idx + 1} entre os grupos avaliados`,
   74 |       ],
   75 |       signals: {
   76 |         aparicoes: x.aparicoes,
   77 |         rankPosition: idx + 1,
   78 |         engine: "score_engine_v1",
   79 |       },
   80 |     };
   81 |   });
   82 | }
   83 | 
   84 | module.exports = {
   85 |   rankingToPredictions,
   86 | };

   42 |     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
   43 |   }

   53 | function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
   54 |   const collected = collectEvidence({
   55 |     item,
   56 |     context,
   57 |     config: scoreConfig,
   58 |   });
   59 | 
   60 |   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
   61 | 
   62 |   return {
   63 |     ...item,
   64 |     score: strength.score,
   65 |     confidence: strength.confidence,
   66 |     reasons: strength.reasons,
   67 |     signals: strength.signals,
   68 |     evidenceCount: collected.count,
   69 |     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
   70 |   };
   71 | }
   72 | 
   73 | function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
   74 | 
   75 |   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
   76 |     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
   77 |     .sort((a, b) => {
   78 | 
   79 |       if (b.score !== a.score)
   80 |         return b.score - a.score;
   81 | 
   82 |       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
   83 |         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
   84 | 
   85 |       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
   86 | 
   87 |     });
   88 | 
   89 | }
   90 | 
   91 | module.exports = {
   92 |   scoreItem,
   93 |   scoreRanking,
   94 |   calculateEvidenceStrength,
   95 | };

  139 | }
  140 | 
  141 | function normalizeDraw(draw = {}) {
  142 |   const drawId = String(
  143 |     draw.drawId ||
  144 |     draw.id ||
  145 |     ""
  146 |   ).trim();
  147 | 
  148 |   const ymd = normalizeYmd(
  149 |     draw.ymd ||
  150 |     draw.date
  151 |   );
  152 | 
  153 |   const closeHour = String(
  154 |     draw.close_hour ||
  155 |     draw.closeHour ||
  156 |     draw.hour ||
  157 |     ""
  158 |   ).trim();
  159 | 
  160 |   if (!drawId || !ymd || !closeHour) {
  161 |     return null;
  162 |   }
  163 | 
  164 |   const prizes = safeArray(draw.prizes)
  165 |     .map(normalizePrize)
  166 |     .filter(Boolean)
  167 |     .sort(
  168 |       (a, b) =>
  169 |         Number(a.position) -
  170 |         Number(b.position)
  171 |     );
  172 | 
  173 |   return {
  174 |     drawId,
  175 |     id: drawId,
  176 |     ymd,
  177 |     closeHour,
  178 |     lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(
  179 |       draw.lottery_key ||
  180 |       draw.lotteryKey ||
  181 |       "PT_RIO"
  182 |     ),
  183 |     lotteryCode:
  184 |       draw.lottery_code ||
  185 |       draw.lotteryCode ||
  186 |       null,
  187 |     prizes,
  188 |   };
  189 | }
  190 | 
  191 | function sortDraws(draws = []) {
  192 |   return [...draws].sort((a, b) => {
  193 |     const aKey =
  194 |       `${a.ymd}T${a.closeHour}T${a.drawId}`;
  195 | 
  196 |     const bKey =
  197 |       `${b.ymd}T${b.closeHour}T${b.drawId}`;
  198 | 
  199 |     return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
  200 |   });
  201 | }
  202 | 
  203 | function deduplicateDraws(draws = []) {
  204 |   const map = new Map();
  205 | 
  206 |   for (const raw of safeArray(draws)) {
  207 |     const draw = normalizeDraw(raw);
  208 | 
  209 |     if (!draw) {
  210 |       continue;
  211 |     }
  212 | 
  213 |     map.set(draw.drawId, draw);
  214 |   }
  215 | 
  216 |   return sortDraws(
  217 |     Array.from(map.values())
  218 |   );
  219 | }
  220 |

  145 | function summarizeMonths(months = []) {
  146 |   const ordered = [...safeArray(months)]
  147 |     .sort((a, b) =>
  148 |       String(a.yearMonth || a.id)
  149 |         .localeCompare(
  150 |           String(b.yearMonth || b.id)

  155 |     ordered.flatMap(
  156 |       (month) =>
  157 |         safeArray(month.draws)
  158 |     )
  159 |   );
  160 | 
  161 |   const first = allDraws[0] || null;
  162 |   const last =
  163 |     allDraws[allDraws.length - 1] || null;
  164 | 
  165 |   return {
  166 |     totalDraws: allDraws.length,
  167 |     monthCount: ordered.length,
  168 |     months: ordered.map(
  169 |       (month) =>
  170 |         month.yearMonth || month.id
  171 |     ),

  173 |     lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,
  174 |     firstDrawId:
  175 |       first?.drawId || null,
  176 |     lastDrawId:
  177 |       last?.drawId || null,
  178 |   };

  693 | function mapTop3ToPredictions(top = []) {
  694 |   return (Array.isArray(top) ? top : [])
  695 |     .slice(0, 3)
  696 |     .map((item, index) => {
  697 |       const grupoNumber = Number(item?.grupo);

  701 |         item?.scoreProb ??
  702 |         item?.probability ??
  703 |         item?.confidence ??
  704 |         0
  705 |       );
  706 | 
  707 |       const percentage =
  708 |         probability > 0 && probability <= 1
  709 |           ? probability * 100
  710 |           : probability;
  711 | 
  712 |       return {
  713 |         type: "grupo",
  714 |         grupo,
  715 |         animal:
  716 |           item?.animal ||
  717 |           ANIMALS[grupo] ||
  718 |           null,
  719 |         score: Number(
  720 |           Number.isFinite(percentage)
  721 |             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
  722 |             : 0
  723 |         ),
  724 |         confidence: Number(
  725 |           Number.isFinite(percentage)
  726 |             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
  727 |             : 0
  728 |         ),

  733 |           engine: "top3_statistical_v3",

  736 |           rawScore: Number(item?.score || 0),
  737 |           meta: item?.meta || null,
  738 |         },
  739 |       };
  740 |     })

  742 | }

   13 | function normalizeLotteryKey(v) {
   14 |   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
   15 | 
   16 |   if (s === "RJ" || s === "RIO" || s === "PT-RIO" || s === "PT_RIO") return "PT_RIO";
   17 |   if (s === "FED" || s === "FEDERAL" || s === "BR") return "FEDERAL";
   18 | 
   19 |   return "";
   20 | }
   21 | 
   22 | /* =========================
   23 |    REGRAS DE HORÁRIOS
   24 | ========================= */
   25 | function getExpectedHours(lottery, date, hasFederal) {
   26 |   if (lottery !== "PT_RIO") return [];
   27 | 
   28 |   const dt = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00`);
   29 |   const dow = dt.getDay(); // 0 = domingo
   30 | 
   31 |   // Domingo: só 09, 11, 14, 16
   32 |   if (dow === 0) {
   33 |     return ["09:00", "11:00", "14:00", "16:00"];
   34 |   }
   35 | 
   36 |   // Base fixa todos os outros dias
   37 |   const expected = ["09:00", "11:00", "14:00", "16:00"];
   38 | 
   39 |   // 21h existe fora domingo
   40 |   expected.push("21:00");
   41 | 
   42 |   // 18h só se NÃO tiver Federal no dia
   43 |   if (!hasFederal) {
   44 |     expected.push("18:00");
   45 |   }
   46 | 
   47 |   return expected.sort();
   48 | }

   50 | function checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal) {
   51 |   const expectedHours = getExpectedHours(lottery, date, hasFederal);
   52 | 
   53 |   if (!expectedHours.length) {
   54 |     return {
   55 |       ok: true,
   56 |       missing: [],
   57 |       extra: [],
   58 |       expectedHours: [],
   59 |       hasFederal: Boolean(hasFederal),
   60 |     };
   61 |   }

   64 |     .map((d) => String(d?.close_hour || "").trim())

   73 |     );
   74 |   }
   75 | 
   76 |   return {
   77 |     ok: missing.length === 0 && extra.length === 0,
   78 |     missing,
   79 |     extra,
   80 |     expectedHours,
   81 |     hasFederal: Boolean(hasFederal),

   88 | function isISODate(s) {
   89 |   const str = String(s || "").trim();
   90 |   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
   91 | 
   92 |   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
   93 |   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   94 | 
   95 |   return (
   96 |     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
   97 |     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
   98 |     dt.getUTCDate() === d
   99 |   );
  100 | }

  102 | function isHHMM(s) {
  103 |   const str = String(s || "").trim();
  104 |   const m = str.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
  105 |   if (!m) return false;
  106 | 
  107 |   const hh = Number(m[1]);
  108 |   const mm = Number(m[2]);
  109 | 
  110 |   return hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59;
  111 | }

  188 | function parsePositionsParam(v) {
  189 |   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  190 |   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
  191 | 
  192 |   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
  193 |   if (m) {
  194 |     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
  195 |     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
  196 |     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
  197 |     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
  198 |     const positions = [];
  199 |     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
  200 |     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
  201 |   }
  202 | 
  203 |   const parts = raw
  204 |     .split(",")
  205 |     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
  206 |     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
  207 | 
  208 |   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
  209 | 
  210 |   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  211 |   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
  212 | }

  231 | async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
  232 |   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  233 |   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
  234 |   const results = new Array(arr.length);
  235 |   let idx = 0;
  236 | 
  237 |   async function worker() {
  238 |     while (true) {
  239 |       const current = idx;
  240 |       idx += 1;
  241 |       if (current >= arr.length) break;
  242 |       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
  243 |     }
  244 |   }
  245 | 
  246 |   await Promise.all(
  247 |     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
  248 |   );
  249 | 
  250 |   return results;
  251 | }

  482 | async function handleRange(req, res) {
  483 |   try {
  484 |     const db = getDb();
  485 | 
  486 |     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
  487 |     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();

  489 |

  491 | 
  492 |     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
  493 |       return res.status(400).json({
  494 |         ok: false,
  495 |         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
  496 |       });
  497 |     }
  498 | 
  499 |     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
  500 |       return res.status(400).json({
  501 |         ok: false,
  502 |         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
  503 |       });
  504 |     }
  505 | 
  506 |     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
  507 |     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
  508 | 
  509 |     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
  510 |     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
  511 | 
  512 |     const snap = await db
  513 |       .collection("draws")
  514 |       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
  515 |       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
  516 |       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
  517 |       .get();
  518 | 
  519 |     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
  520 |     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
  521 |     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
  522 |     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
  523 | 
  524 |     const groupedIntegrity = {};
  525 |     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
  526 |       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
  527 |         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
  528 |         if (!ymd) return acc;
  529 |         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
  530 |         acc[ymd].push(d);
  531 |         return acc;
  532 |       }, {});
  533 | 
  534 |       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
  535 |         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
  536 |         if (!ymd) return acc;
  537 |         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
  538 |         acc[ymd].push(d);
  539 |         return acc;
  540 |       }, {});
  541 | 
  542 |       const allDays = Array.from(
  543 |         new Set([
  544 |           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
  545 |           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
  546 |         ])
  547 |       ).sort();
  548 | 
  549 |       for (const ymd of allDays) {
  550 |         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
  551 |         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
  552 |         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
  553 |         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
  554 |       }
  555 |     }
  556 | 
  557 |     return res.json({
  558 |       ok: true,
  559 |       mode: "range",
  560 |       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
  561 |       dateFrom,
  562 |       dateTo,
  563 |       lottery,
  564 |       from: win.from || null,
  565 |       to: win.to || null,
  566 |       includePrizes,
  567 |       count: draws.length,
  568 |       draws,
  569 |     });
  570 |   } catch (e) {

  572 |   }
  573 | }

  321 | function normalizeHourList(arr) {
  322 |   const out = [];
  323 |   const seen = new Set();
  324 |   for (const v of Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : []) {
  325 |     const m = String(v ?? "").match(/\d{1,2}/);
  326 |     if (!m) continue;
  327 |     const hh = pad2(Number(m[0]));

  330 |     if (!seen.has(hh)) {
  331 |       seen.add(hh);
  332 |       out.push(hh);
  333 |     }
  334 |   }
  335 |   return out.sort();
  336 | }

  359 |   }

  367 | function pickRangeForDate(ranges, ymd) {
  368 |   for (const r of Array.isArray(ranges) ? ranges : []) {
  369 |     const from0 = String(r?.from || "").trim();
  370 |     const to0 = String(r?.to || "").trim();
  371 | 
  372 |     const hasFrom = isISODate(from0);
  373 |     const hasTo = isISODate(to0);
  374 | 
  375 |     if (!hasFrom && !hasTo) return r;
  376 | 
  377 |     if (!hasFrom && hasTo) {
  378 |       if (ymd <= to0) return r;
  379 |       continue;
  380 |     }
  381 |     if (hasFrom && !hasTo) {
  382 |       if (ymd >= from0) return r;
  383 |       continue;
  384 |     }
  385 | 
  386 |     if (hasFrom && hasTo && ymd >= from0 && ymd <= to0) return r;
  387 |   }
  388 |   return null;
  389 | }

  216 | function loadScheduleOrNull(lotteryKey, scheduleFileArg) {
  217 |   const scheduleFile =
  218 |     String(scheduleFileArg || "").trim() ||
  219 |     path.join(DEFAULT_SCHEDULE_DIR, `${lotteryKey}.json`);
  220 | 
  221 |   if (!fs.existsSync(scheduleFile)) return { schedule: null, scheduleFile };
  222 | 
  223 |   const schedule = safeReadJson(scheduleFile, null);
  224 |   if (!schedule || typeof schedule !== "object") return { schedule: null, scheduleFile };
  225 | 
  226 |   const lk = String(schedule.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  227 |   if (lk && lk !== lotteryKey) {
  228 |     return { schedule: null, scheduleFile };
  229 |   }
  230 | 
  231 |   const ranges = Array.isArray(schedule.ranges) ? schedule.ranges : [];
  232 |   schedule.lotteryKey = lotteryKey;
  233 |   schedule.ranges = ranges
  234 |     .filter((r) => r && typeof r === "object" && isISODate(r.from))
  235 |     .map((r) => ({
  236 |       from: String(r.from).trim(),
  237 |       to: r.to == null || r.to === "" ? null : String(r.to).trim(),
  238 |       dow: r.dow && typeof r.dow === "object" ? r.dow : {},
  239 |     }))
  240 |     .sort((a, b) => (a.from < b.from ? -1 : a.from > b.from ? 1 : 0));
  241 | 
  242 |   return { schedule, scheduleFile };
  243 | }
  244 | 
  245 | function pickRangeForDate(schedule, ymd) {
  246 |   if (!schedule || !Array.isArray(schedule.ranges)) return null;
  247 |   let picked = null;
  248 |   for (const r of schedule.ranges) {
  249 |     if (!r || !r.from) continue;
  250 |     if (r.from <= ymd && (r.to == null || ymd <= r.to)) {
  251 |       picked = r;
  252 |     }
  253 |   }
  254 |   return picked;
  255 | }

  257 | function expectedHoursFromSchedule(schedule, ymd, dow) {
  258 |   const r = pickRangeForDate(schedule, ymd);
  259 |   if (!r) return null;
  260 | 
  261 |   const dd = String(dow);

  266 |       expectedHard: [],

  269 |       range: { from: r.from, to: r.to },
  270 |     };
  271 |   }

  274 |   const expectedSoft = normalizeHourList(rule.soft);
  275 |

  280 |     range: { from: r.from, to: r.to },

  284 | // =====================
  285 | // ✅ SCHEDULE DIGEST (não infla JSON)
  286 | // =====================
  287 | function buildScheduleDigestFromFileOrNull(scheduleFile) {
  288 |   try {
  289 |     const sf = String(scheduleFile || "").trim();
  290 |     if (!sf || !fs.existsSync(sf)) return null;
  291 | 
  292 |     const raw = safeReadJson(sf, null);
  293 |     if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
  294 | 
  295 |     const ranges = Array.isArray(raw.ranges) ? raw.ranges : [];
  296 |     const oneoffs = [];
  297 |     let hardSlotsTotal = 0;
  298 |     let softSlotsTotal = 0;
  299 | 
  300 |     for (const r of ranges) {
  301 |       if (r && r.from && r.to && String(r.from) === String(r.to) && isISODate(r.from)) {
  302 |         oneoffs.push(String(r.from));
  303 |       }
  304 | 
  305 |       const dow = r && typeof r.dow === "object" && r.dow ? r.dow : {};
  306 |       for (const k of Object.keys(dow)) {
  307 |         const obj = dow[k] || {};
  308 |         const h = Array.isArray(obj.hard) ? obj.hard : [];
  309 |         const s = Array.isArray(obj.soft) ? obj.soft : [];
  310 |         hardSlotsTotal += h.length;
  311 |         softSlotsTotal += s.length;
  312 |       }
  313 |     }
  314 | 
  315 |     const stat = fs.statSync(sf);
  316 |     const uniqOneoffs = Array.from(new Set(oneoffs)).sort();
  317 | 
  318 |     return {
  319 |       rangesCount: ranges.length,
  320 |       oneOffCount: uniqOneoffs.length,
  321 |       oneOffDays: uniqOneoffs,
  322 |       hardSlotsTotal,
  323 |       softSlotsTotal,
  324 |       file: sf,
  325 |       mtimeMs: Number(stat.mtimeMs || 0),
  326 |     };
  327 |   } catch {
  328 |     return null;
  329 |   }
  330 | }

  357 | function gapSetForDate(gaps, ymd) {
  358 |   if (!gaps || !gaps.gapsByDay) return null;
  359 |   const v = gaps.gapsByDay?.[ymd];
  360 |   if (!v) return null;
  361 | 
  362 |   const set = new Set();
  363 | 
  364 |   const pushHour = (h) => {
  365 |     const m = String(h ?? "").match(/\d{1,2}/);
  366 |     if (!m) return;
  367 |     const n = Number(m[0]);
  368 |     if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 23) return;
  369 |     const hh = pad2(n);
  370 |     if (/^\d{2}$/.test(hh)) set.add(hh);
  371 |   };
  372 | 
  373 |   // ✅ formato antigo: gapsByDay[ymd] = ["18","21"]
  374 |   if (Array.isArray(v)) {
  375 |     for (const h of v) pushHour(h);
  376 |     return set.size ? set : null;
  377 |   }
  378 | 
  379 |   // ✅ formato novo: gapsByDay[ymd] = { removedHard:[18], removedSoft:[..] }
  380 |   if (v && typeof v === "object") {
  381 |     const rh = Array.isArray(v.removedHard) ? v.removedHard : [];
  382 |     const rs = Array.isArray(v.removedSoft) ? v.removedSoft : [];
  383 |     for (const h of rh) pushHour(h);
  384 |     for (const h of rs) pushHour(h);
  385 |     return set.size ? set : null;
  386 |   }

  445 | // =====================
  446 | // Time helpers (local)
  447 | // =====================
  448 | function getLocalYmd(d) {
  449 |   const x = d ? new Date(d) : new Date();
  450 |   return `${x.getFullYear()}-${pad2(x.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(x.getDate())}`;
  451 | }
  452 | function getLocalNowMinutes(d) {
  453 |   const x = d ? new Date(d) : new Date();
  454 |   return x.getHours() * 60 + x.getMinutes();
  455 | }
  456 | function hourToMinutes(h) {
  457 |   const n = Number(h);
  458 |   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n * 60 : NaN;
  459 | }
  460 | 
  461 | // ✅ NOVO: parse "HH:mm" -> minutos
  462 | function parseHmToMinutes(hm) {
  463 |   const s = String(hm || "").trim();
  464 |   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
  465 |   if (!m) return null;
  466 |   const hh = Number(m[1]);
  467 |   const mm = Number(m[2]);
  468 |   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return null;
  469 |   if (hh < 0 || hh > 23 || mm < 0 || mm > 59) return null;
  470 |   return hh * 60 + mm;
  471 | }

  476 | async function main() {
  477 |   const lotteryKey = String(process.argv[2] || "PT_RIO").trim().toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
  478 |   const startYmd = String(process.argv[3] || "2022-06-07").trim();
  479 |

  482 |     return;

  484 |

  124 | function auditCountsFromDocs(rows) {
  125 |   const setYmd = new Set();
  126 |   const setYmdHour = new Set();
  127 |   const mapKeyCount = new Map();
  128 |   let invalidYmd = 0;
  129 | 
  130 |   for (const r of rows) {
  131 |     const { ymd, hour } = extractYmdAndHour(r);
  132 |     if (!ymd) { invalidYmd++; continue; }
  133 |     setYmd.add(ymd);
  134 | 
  135 |     const key = `${ymd}__${hour || ""}`;
  136 |     mapKeyCount.set(key, (mapKeyCount.get(key) || 0) + 1);
  137 |     if (hour) setYmdHour.add(key);
  138 |   }
  139 | 
  140 |   const dupKeys = [...mapKeyCount.entries()]
  141 |     .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
  142 |     .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  143 | 
  144 |   return {
  145 |     docs_brutos: rows.length,
  146 |     unique_ymd: setYmd.size,
  147 |     unique_ymd_hour: setYmdHour.size,
  148 |     invalid_ymd: invalidYmd,
  149 |     keys_total: mapKeyCount.size,
  150 |     keys_duplicadas: dupKeys.length,
  151 |     top_duplicadas: dupKeys.slice(0, 15),
  152 |   };
  153 | }

  155 | async function fetchRowsForRange(db, { lotteryKey, fromYmd, toYmd, pageSize = 2000, maxPages = 80 } = {}) {
  156 |   const lot = lotteryKey ? String(lotteryKey).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
  157 | 
  158 |   // 1) tentativa indexada (aceita range parcial)
  159 |   try {
  160 |     let q = db.collection("draws");
  161 |     if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
  162 |     if (fromYmd) q = q.where("ymd", ">=", fromYmd);
  163 |     if (toYmd) q = q.where("ymd", "<=", toYmd);
  164 | 
  165 |     const snap = await q.get();
  166 |     const rows = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...(d.data() || {}) }));
  167 |     return { rows, source: "query: where(lottery_key)+where(ymd>=/<=)", usedFallback: false };
  168 |   } catch (e) {
  169 |     if (!isIndexError(e)) throw e;
  170 |   }
  171 | 
  172 |   // 2) fallback sem índice: pagina por docId e filtra em memória
  173 |   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
  174 |   let q = db.collection("draws");
  175 |   if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
  176 |   q = q.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc");
  177 | 
  178 |   let last = null;
  179 |   const out = [];
  180 | 
  181 |   for (let page = 1; page <= maxPages; page++) {
  182 |     let qq = q.limit(pageSize);
  183 |     if (last) qq = qq.startAfter(last);
  184 | 
  185 |     const snap = await qq.get();
  186 |     if (snap.empty) break;
  187 | 
  188 |     for (const doc of snap.docs) {
  189 |       const data = doc.data() || {};
  190 |       const ymd = normalizeToYMD(data.ymd ?? data.date ?? data.draw_date ?? data.close_date ?? data.dt ?? data.data);
  191 |       if (!ymd || !isISODateStrict(ymd)) continue;
  192 |       if (fromYmd && ymd < fromYmd) continue;
  193 |       if (toYmd && ymd > toYmd) continue;
  194 |       out.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
  195 |     }
  196 | 
  197 |     last = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
  198 |   }
  199 | 
  200 |   return { rows: out, source: "fallback: paginate by docId + filter(ymd)", usedFallback: true };
  201 | }
  202 | 
  203 | function parseArg(name, def = null) {
  204 |   const i = process.argv.findIndex((x) => x === name);
  205 |   if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] != null) return process.argv[i + 1];
  206 |   return def;
  207 | }

   98 | function pickBestDoc(arr) {
   99 |   const list = Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.slice() : [];
  100 |   list.sort((a, b) => Number(b.prizeCount || 0) - Number(a.prizeCount || 0));
  101 |   return list[0] || null;
  102 | }

  104 | async function auditDate(date) {
  105 |   const payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY });
  106 |   const sourceDraws = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
  107 | 
  108 |   const sourceBySlot = new Map();
  109 | 
  110 |   for (const draw of sourceDraws) {
  111 |     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForFederal(draw?.close_hour || "");
  112 |     if (!slot) continue;
  113 |     sourceBySlot.set(slot, {
  114 |       slot,
  115 |       prizes: extractKingPrizes(draw),
  116 |     });
  117 |   }
  118 | 
  119 |   const fsBySlot = await readFirestoreByDate(date);
  120 | 
  121 |   const allSlots = Array.from(
  122 |     new Set([...sourceBySlot.keys(), ...fsBySlot.keys()])
  123 |   ).sort();
  124 | 
  125 |   const problems = [];
  126 | 
  127 |   for (const slot of allSlots) {
  128 |     const src = sourceBySlot.get(slot);
  129 |     const docs = fsBySlot.get(slot) || [];
  130 |     const fsDoc = pickBestDoc(docs);
  131 | 
  132 |     if (!src) {
  133 |       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "extra_firestore", firestoreDocs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
  134 |       continue;
  135 |     }
  136 | 
  137 |     if (!fsDoc) {
  138 |       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "missing_firestore" });
  139 |       continue;
  140 |     }
  141 | 
  142 |     if (docs.length > 1) {
  143 |       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "duplicate_firestore", count: docs.length, docs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
  144 |     }
  145 | 
  146 |     for (let pos = 1; pos <= 7; pos++) {
  147 |       const a = src.prizes[pos] || "";
  148 |       const b = fsDoc.prizes[pos] || "";
  149 | 
  150 |       if (!a || !b || a !== b) {
  151 |         problems.push({
  152 |           date,
  153 |           slot,
  154 |           type: "prize_mismatch",
  155 |           position: pos,
  156 |           source: a,
  157 |           firestore: b,
  158 |           firestoreDoc: fsDoc.id,
  159 |         });
  160 |       }
  161 |     }
  162 |   }
  163 | 
  164 |   return {
  165 |     date,
  166 |     sourceSlots: Array.from(sourceBySlot.keys()).sort(),
  167 |     firestoreSlots: Array.from(fsBySlot.keys()).sort(),
  168 |     problems,
  169 |   };
  170 | }
  171 | 
  172 | async function main() {
  173 |   const started = Date.now();
  174 |   const results = [];
  175 |   let totalDays = 0;
  176 |   let totalSlots = 0;
  177 |   let totalProblems = 0;
  178 | 
  179 |   for (let d = START; d <= END; d = addDays(d, 1)) {
  180 |     totalDays++;
  181 | 
  182 |     try {
  183 |       const r = await auditDate(d);
  184 |       results.push(r);
  185 |       totalSlots += r.sourceSlots.length;
  186 |       totalProblems += r.problems.length;
  187 | 
  188 |       const status = r.problems.length ? "PROBLEM" : "OK";
  189 |       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ${status} sourceSlots=${r.sourceSlots.length} problems=${r.problems.length}`);
  190 | 
  191 |       if (r.problems.length) {
  192 |         console.log(JSON.stringify(r.problems.slice(0, 10), null, 2));
  193 |       }
  194 |     } catch (e) {
  195 |       totalProblems++;
  196 |       const r = {
  197 |         date: d,
  198 |         sourceSlots: [],
  199 |         firestoreSlots: [],
  200 |         problems: [{ date: d, type: "audit_error", message: String(e?.message || e) }],
  201 |       };
  202 |       results.push(r);
  203 |       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ERROR ${String(e?.message || e)}`);
  204 |     }
  205 |   }
  206 | 
  207 |   const out = {
  208 |     period: { start: START, end: END },
  209 |     lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY,
  210 |     totalDays,
  211 |     totalSourceSlots: totalSlots,
  212 |     totalProblems,
  213 |     ok: totalProblems === 0,
  214 |     tookMs: Date.now() - started,
  215 |     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  216 |     results,
  217 |   };
  218 | 
  219 |   const dir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
  220 |   fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  221 | 
  222 |   const file = path.join(dir, `content-audit-${LOTTERY_KEY}-${START}_to_${END}.json`);
  223 |   fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), "utf8");
  224 | 
  225 |   console.log("");
  226 |   console.log("========== AUDITORIA DE CONTEÚDO ==========");
  227 |   console.log(`Período: ${START} até ${END}`);
  228 |   console.log(`Loteria: ${LOTTERY_KEY}`);
  229 |   console.log(`Dias auditados: ${totalDays}`);
  230 |   console.log(`Sorteios fonte: ${totalSlots}`);
  231 |   console.log(`Problemas: ${totalProblems}`);
  232 |   console.log(`OK: ${out.ok ? "SIM" : "NÃO"}`);
  233 |   console.log(`Arquivo: ${file}`);
  234 | 
  235 |   process.exit(out.ok ? 0 : 2);
  236 | }

  158 | async function main() {
  159 |   const official = await fetchOfficialFederal();
  160 |   const firestore = await readFederalFirestoreDocs();
  161 | 
  162 |   const officialByKey = new Map();
  163 |   const firestoreByKey = new Map();
  164 | 
  165 |   for (const r of official) {
  166 |     const k = keyFor(r);
  167 |     if (!officialByKey.has(k)) officialByKey.set(k, []);
  168 |     officialByKey.get(k).push(r);
  169 |   }
  170 | 
  171 |   for (const r of firestore) {
  172 |     const k = keyFor(r);
  173 |     if (!firestoreByKey.has(k)) firestoreByKey.set(k, []);
  174 |     firestoreByKey.get(k).push(r);
  175 |   }
  176 | 
  177 |   const allKeys = new Set([...officialByKey.keys(), ...firestoreByKey.keys()]);
  178 |   const rows = [];
  179 | 
  180 |   let ok = 0;
  181 |   let missing = 0;
  182 |   let extra = 0;
  183 |   let divergent = 0;
  184 |   let duplicated = 0;
  185 | 
  186 |   for (const k of Array.from(allKeys).sort()) {
  187 |     const src = officialByKey.get(k) || [];
  188 |     const fsRows = firestoreByKey.get(k) || [];
  189 | 
  190 |     const srcBest = src[0] || null;
  191 |     const fsBest = fsRows[0] || null;
  192 | 
  193 |     let status = "ok";
  194 | 
  195 |     if (src.length && !fsRows.length) {
  196 |       status = "missing_firestore";
  197 |       missing++;
  198 |     } else if (!src.length && fsRows.length) {
  199 |       status = "extra_firestore";
  200 |       extra++;
  201 |     } else if (src.length && fsRows.length) {
  202 |       if (fsRows.length > 1) duplicated++;
  203 | 
  204 |       const hasMatchingContent = fsRows.some((f) => samePrizes(srcBest.prizes, f.prizes));
  205 | 
  206 |       if (!hasMatchingContent) {
  207 |         status = "conteudo_divergente";
  208 |         divergent++;
  209 |       } else {
  210 |         status = fsRows.length > 1 ? "ok_com_duplicidade" : "ok";
  211 |         ok++;
  212 |       }
  213 |     }
  214 | 
  215 |     rows.push({
  216 |       key: k,
  217 |       status,
  218 |       officialCount: src.length,
  219 |       firestoreCount: fsRows.length,
  220 |       official: src.map((x) => ({
  221 |         date: x.date,
  222 |         hour: x.hour,
  223 |         contest: x.contest,
  224 |         hash: x.hash,
  225 |         prizeCount: x.prizeCount,
  226 |         prizes: x.prizes,
  227 |       })),
  228 |       firestore: fsRows.map((x) => ({
  229 |         id: x.id,
  230 |         date: x.date,
  231 |         hour: x.hour,
  232 |         contest: x.contest,
  233 |         hash: x.hash,
  234 |         prizeCount: x.prizeCount,
  235 |         prizes: x.prizes,
  236 |       })),
  237 |     });
  238 |   }
  239 | 
  240 |   const report = {
  241 |     period: { start: START, end: END },
  242 |     lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY,
  243 |     officialResults: official.length,
  244 |     officialUniqueKeys: officialByKey.size,
  245 |     firestoreDocs: firestore.length,
  246 |     firestoreUniqueKeys: firestoreByKey.size,
  247 |     ok,
  248 |     missing,
  249 |     extra,
  250 |     divergent,
  251 |     duplicatedKeys: duplicated,
  252 |     problems: rows.filter((r) => r.status !== "ok"),
  253 |     rows,
  254 |     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  255 |   };

   74 | function keyFor(row) {

   76 |     ? row.prizes
   77 |     : Object.values(row.prizes || {});

   79 |   const normalized = prizes
   80 |     .map((p) => String(p || "").padStart(4, "0"))
   81 |     .join("|");
   82 | 
   83 |   return `${row.date}::${normalized}`;
   84 | }

   88 |   let score = 0;

   93 |   if (d.lotteryKey === "FEDERAL") score += 20;
   94 |   if (d.source) score += 5;
   95 |   if (d.createdAt) score += 2;

   44 | async function readFederalFirestoreByDate(date) {
   45 |   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
   46 |   const rows = [];
   47 | 
   48 |   for (const doc of snap.docs) {
   49 |     const d = doc.data() || {};
   50 |     const lk = String(d.lottery_key || d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   51 |     if (lk !== LOTTERY_KEY) continue;
   52 | 
   53 |     const pSnap = await doc.ref.collection("prizes").get();
   54 |     const prizes = {};
   55 | 
   56 |     pSnap.forEach((pdoc) => {
   57 |       const p = pdoc.data() || {};

   59 |       const milhar = normMilhar(p.milhar || p.value || p.raw);
   60 |       if (pos >= 1 && pos <= 7 && milhar) prizes[pos] = milhar;
   61 |     });

   64 |       id: doc.id,
   65 |       close_hour: String(d.close_hour || d.closeHour || d.hour || "").trim(),
   66 |       prizeCount: Object.keys(prizes).length,
   67 |       prizes,
   68 |     });
   69 |   }
   70 |

   16 | function normHour(v) {
   17 |   const s = String(v || "").trim();
   18 |   const m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})[:hH]?(\d{2})?/);
   19 |   if (!m) return "";
   20 |   return String(Number(m[1])).padStart(2, "0") + ":00";
   21 | }
   22 |

   59 | function safeReadJson(filePath, fallback) {
   60 |   try {
   61 |     if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return fallback;
   62 |     const raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
   63 |     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
   64 |     return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed : fallback;
   65 |   } catch {
   66 |     return fallback;
   67 |   }
   68 | }
   69 | 
   70 | function normalizeHourList(arr) {
   71 |   const out = [];
   72 |   const seen = new Set();
   73 | 
   74 |   for (const v of Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : []) {
   75 |     const m = String(v ?? "").match(/\d{1,2}/);
   76 |     if (!m) continue;
   77 | 
   78 |     const hh = pad2(Number(m[0]));
   79 |     const n = Number(hh);
   80 |     if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 23) continue;
   81 | 
   82 |     if (!seen.has(hh)) {
   83 |       seen.add(hh);
   84 |       out.push(hh);
   85 |     }
   86 |   }
   87 | 
   88 |   return out.sort();
   89 | }
   90 | 
   91 | function uniqSorted(arr) {
   92 |   return Array.from(new Set(Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [])).sort();
   93 | }
   94 | 
   95 | function pickRangeForDate(ranges, ymd) {
   96 |   for (const r of Array.isArray(ranges) ? ranges : []) {
   97 |     const from0 = String(r?.from || "").trim();
   98 |     const to0 = String(r?.to || "").trim();
   99 | 
  100 |     const hasFrom = isISODate(from0);
  101 |     const hasTo = isISODate(to0);
  102 | 
  103 |     if (!hasFrom && !hasTo) return r;
  104 |     if (!hasFrom && hasTo && ymd <= to0) return r;
  105 |     if (hasFrom && !hasTo && ymd >= from0) return r;
  106 |     if (hasFrom && hasTo && ymd >= from0 && ymd <= to0) return r;
  107 |   }
  108 | 
  109 |   return null;
  110 | }

  127 | function getExpectedForDate(ymd, slotSchedule) {
  128 |   const ranges = Array.isArray(slotSchedule) ? slotSchedule : slotSchedule?.ranges;
  129 |   const r = pickRangeForDate(ranges, ymd);
  130 | 
  131 |   if (!r || !r.dow || typeof r.dow !== "object") {
  132 |     return { hard: [], soft: [], source: "none" };
  133 |   }
  134 | 
  135 |   const d = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00-03:00`);
  136 |   const dow = String(d.getDay());
  137 |   const block = r.dow[dow] || r.dow[Number(dow)] || null;
  138 | 
  139 |   if (!block) {
  140 |     return { hard: [], soft: [], source: "none" };
  141 |   }
  142 | 
  143 |   return {
  144 |     hard: normalizeHourList(block.hard || []),
  145 |     soft: normalizeHourList(block.soft || []),
  146 |     source: "slot_schedule",
  147 |   };
  148 | }

  150 | function getGapsForDate(ymd, sourceGaps) {
  151 |   const entry = sourceGaps?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? sourceGaps?.[ymd] ?? null;
  152 |   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
  153 | 
  154 |   return {

   98 | function pickBestDoc(arr) {
   99 |   const list = Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.slice() : [];
  100 |   list.sort((a, b) => Number(b.prizeCount || 0) - Number(a.prizeCount || 0));
  101 |   return list[0] || null;

  104 | async function auditDate(date) {
  105 |   const payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY });
  106 |   const sourceDraws = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
  107 | 
  108 |   const sourceBySlot = new Map();
  109 | 
  110 |   for (const draw of sourceDraws) {
  111 |     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForPtRio(draw?.close_hour || "");
  112 |     if (!slot) continue;
  113 |     sourceBySlot.set(slot, {
  114 |       slot,
  115 |       prizes: extractKingPrizes(draw),
  116 |     });
  117 |   }
  118 | 
  119 |   const fsBySlot = await readFirestoreByDate(date);
  120 | 
  121 |   const allSlots = Array.from(
  122 |     new Set([...sourceBySlot.keys(), ...fsBySlot.keys()])
  123 |   ).sort();
  124 | 
  125 |   const problems = [];
  126 | 
  127 |   for (const slot of allSlots) {
  128 |     const src = sourceBySlot.get(slot);
  129 |     const docs = fsBySlot.get(slot) || [];
  130 |     const fsDoc = pickBestDoc(docs);
  131 | 
  132 |     if (!src) {
  133 |       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "extra_firestore", firestoreDocs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
  134 |       continue;
  135 |     }
  136 | 
  137 |     if (!fsDoc) {
  138 |       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "missing_firestore" });
  139 |       continue;
  140 |     }
  141 | 
  142 |     if (docs.length > 1) {
  143 |       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "duplicate_firestore", count: docs.length, docs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
  144 |     }
  145 | 
  146 |     for (let pos = 1; pos <= 7; pos++) {
  147 |       const a = src.prizes[pos] || "";
  148 |       const b = fsDoc.prizes[pos] || "";
  149 | 
  150 |       if (!a || !b || a !== b) {
  151 |         problems.push({
  152 |           date,
  153 |           slot,
  154 |           type: "prize_mismatch",
  155 |           position: pos,
  156 |           source: a,
  157 |           firestore: b,
  158 |           firestoreDoc: fsDoc.id,
  159 |         });
  160 |       }
  161 |     }
  162 |   }
  163 | 
  164 |   return {
  165 |     date,
  166 |     sourceSlots: Array.from(sourceBySlot.keys()).sort(),
  167 |     firestoreSlots: Array.from(fsBySlot.keys()).sort(),
  168 |     problems,
  169 |   };
  170 | }

  172 | async function main() {
  173 |   const started = Date.now();
  174 |   const results = [];
  175 |   let totalDays = 0;
  176 |   let totalSlots = 0;
  177 |   let totalProblems = 0;
  178 | 
  179 |   for (let d = START; d <= END; d = addDays(d, 1)) {
  180 |     totalDays++;
  181 | 
  182 |     try {
  183 |       const r = await auditDate(d);
  184 |       results.push(r);
  185 |       totalSlots += r.sourceSlots.length;
  186 |       totalProblems += r.problems.length;
  187 | 
  188 |       const status = r.problems.length ? "PROBLEM" : "OK";
  189 |       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ${status} sourceSlots=${r.sourceSlots.length} problems=${r.problems.length}`);
  190 | 
  191 |       if (r.problems.length) {
  192 |         console.log(JSON.stringify(r.problems.slice(0, 10), null, 2));
  193 |       }
  194 |     } catch (e) {
  195 |       totalProblems++;
  196 |       const r = {
  197 |         date: d,
  198 |         sourceSlots: [],
  199 |         firestoreSlots: [],
  200 |         problems: [{ date: d, type: "audit_error", message: String(e?.message || e) }],
  201 |       };
  202 |       results.push(r);
  203 |       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ERROR ${String(e?.message || e)}`);
  204 |     }
  205 |   }
  206 | 
  207 |   const out = {
  208 |     period: { start: START, end: END },
  209 |     lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY,
  210 |     totalDays,
  211 |     totalSourceSlots: totalSlots,
  212 |     totalProblems,
  213 |     ok: totalProblems === 0,
  214 |     tookMs: Date.now() - started,
  215 |     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  216 |     results,
  217 |   };
  218 | 
  219 |   const dir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
  220 |   fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  221 | 
  222 |   const file = path.join(dir, `content-audit-${LOTTERY_KEY}-${START}_to_${END}.json`);
  223 |   fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), "utf8");
  224 | 
  225 |   console.log("");
  226 |   console.log("========== AUDITORIA DE CONTEÚDO ==========");
  227 |   console.log(`Período: ${START} até ${END}`);
  228 |   console.log(`Loteria: ${LOTTERY_KEY}`);
  229 |   console.log(`Dias auditados: ${totalDays}`);
  230 |   console.log(`Sorteios fonte: ${totalSlots}`);
  231 |   console.log(`Problemas: ${totalProblems}`);
  232 |   console.log(`OK: ${out.ok ? "SIM" : "NÃO"}`);
  233 |   console.log(`Arquivo: ${file}`);
  234 | 
  235 |   process.exit(out.ok ? 0 : 2);
  236 | }

  181 | function pickLogFile({ kind, lottery, start, end }) {
  182 |   const lot = String(lottery || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  183 |   const s = String(start || "").trim();
  184 |   const e = String(end || "").trim();
  185 | 
  186 |   const dir = LOG_DIR;
  187 |   const files = listFilesSafe(dir);
  188 |   if (!files.length) return null;
  189 | 
  190 |   // e.g. auditSlots-PT_RIO-2022-06-07_to_2026-02-28.json
  191 |   const baseRe = new RegExp(
  192 |     `^${kind}-${lot}-\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}_to_\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\.json$`,
  193 |     "i"
  194 |   );
  195 | 
  196 |   const candidates = files
  197 |     .filter((f) => baseRe.test(f))
  198 |     .map((f) => {
  199 |       const m = f.match(
  200 |         new RegExp(`^${kind}-${lot}-(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})_to_(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})\\.json$`, "i")
  201 |       );
  202 |       const fStart = m?.[1] || "";
  203 |       const fEnd = m?.[2] || "";
  204 |       const full = path.join(dir, f);
  205 |       return { f, full, fStart, fEnd, mtimeMs: statMtimeMsSafe(full) };
  206 |     });
  207 | 
  208 |   if (!candidates.length) return null;
  209 | 
  210 |   // 1) match exato
  211 |   const exact = candidates.filter((c) => c.fStart === s && c.fEnd === e);
  212 |   if (exact.length) {
  213 |     exact.sort((a, b) => b.mtimeMs - a.mtimeMs);
  214 |     return exact[0].full;
  215 |   }
  216 | 
  217 |   // 2) fallback: mais recente (do lottery)
  218 |   candidates.sort((a, b) => b.mtimeMs - a.mtimeMs);
  219 |   return candidates[0].full;
  220 | }
  221 | 
  222 | function pickAuditFile(lottery, start, end) {
  223 |   return pickLogFile({ kind: "auditSlots", lottery, start, end });
  224 | }

  226 | function pickPlanFile(lottery, start, end) {
  227 |   return pickLogFile({ kind: "backfillPlan", lottery, start, end });
  228 | }

   25 | function normalizeHHMM(v) {
   26 |   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   27 |   if (!s) return "";
   28 |   if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
   29 |   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   30 |   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   31 |   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   32 |   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   33 |   return "";
   34 | }

   46 |   if (!hasFederal) expected.push("18:00");
   47 |   return expected.sort();

   69 | function nowHHMMLocal() {
   70 |   try {
   71 |     const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   72 |       timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
   73 |       hour: "2-digit",
   74 |       minute: "2-digit",
   75 |       hour12: false,
   76 |     });
   77 |     return fmt.format(new Date()).replace(".", ":");
   78 |   } catch {
   79 |     const d = new Date();
   80 |     return `${pad2(d.getHours())}:${pad2(d.getMinutes())}`;
   81 |   }

   83 | 
   84 | function addMinutesToHHMM(hhmm, minutes) {
   85 |   const [h, m] = String(hhmm || "00:00").split(":").map(Number);
   86 |   const total = (h * 60) + m + Number(minutes || 0);
   87 |   const hh = Math.floor(total / 60);
   88 |   const mm = total % 60;
   89 |   return `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}`;
   90 | }
   91 | 
   92 | function getDuePtRioHours(ymd, hasFederal) {
   93 |   const expected = getExpectedPtRioHours(ymd, hasFederal);
   94 |   const today = todayYMDLocal();
   95 | 
   96 |   if (ymd < today) return expected;
   97 |   if (ymd > today) return [];
   98 | 
   99 |   const now = nowHHMMLocal();
  100 |   const toleranceMin = 10;
  101 | 
  102 |   return expected.filter(h => addMinutesToHHMM(h, toleranceMin) <= now);
  103 | }

   51 | function normalizeHour(value) {
   52 |   const raw = String(value || "")
   53 |     .trim()
   54 |     .replace("h", ":00");
   55 | 
   56 |   if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(raw)) {
   57 |     return `${raw.padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   58 |   }
   59 | 
   60 |   if (/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw)) {
   61 |     const [hour, minute] = raw.split(":");
   62 | 
   63 |     return `${hour.padStart(2, "0")}:${minute}`;
   64 |   }
   65 | 
   66 |   return null;
   67 | }
   68 | 
   69 | function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
   70 |   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour) || hour}`;
   71 | }

  255 |           bYmd,
  256 |           bHour

  803 |   const errors = [];

  805 |   // helper local: extrai e normaliza closes vindos nesse UUID
  806 |   const closeFromDraw = (d) =>
  807 |     normalizeHHMM(d?.close_hour ?? d?.closeHour ?? d?.horario ?? d?.close ?? "");
  808 | 
  809 |   const summarizeCloseHoursPart = (list) => {
  810 |     const set = new Set();
  811 |     for (const d of Array.isArray(list) ? list : []) {
  812 |       const c = closeFromDraw(d);
  813 |       if (c) set.add(c);
  814 |     }
  815 |     return Array.from(set).sort();
  816 |   };
  817 | 
  818 |   for (const lotteryId of lotteries) {
  819 |     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk, lotteryId });
  820 | 
  821 |     try {
  822 |       const data = await axiosGetJson(url);
  823 | 
  824 |       // ✅ carimba o UUID do request em cada draw (a API nem sempre retorna lottery_id)
  825 |       if (data && Array.isArray(data.data)) {
  826 |         data.data = data.data.map((d) => ({
  827 |           ...d,
  828 |           lottery_id: d?.lottery_id || lotteryId,
  829 |           lotteryId: d?.lotteryId || lotteryId,
  830 |         }));
  831 |       }
  832 |       const list = Array.isArray(data?.data) ? data.data : [];
  833 |       parts.push(list);
  834 | 
  835 |       if (KING_FETCH_DEBUG) {
  836 |         const closesPart = summarizeCloseHoursPart(list);
  837 |         console.log(
  838 |           `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} draws=${list.length} close_hours=[${closesPart.join(
  839 |             ", "
  840 |           )}]`
  841 |         );
  842 |       }
  843 |     } catch (e) {
  844 |       const msg = e?.response?.status
  845 |         ? `HTTP_${e.response.status}`
  846 |         : e?.code || e?.message || "ERR";
  847 | 
  848 |       errors.push({ lotteryId, msg });
  849 | 
  850 |       console.warn(
  851 |         `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} ERROR=${msg} (seguindo...)`
  852 |       );
  853 | 
  854 |       // segue para não travar o dia inteiro por 1 UUID
  855 |       continue;
  856 |     }
  857 |   }
  858 | 
  859 |   const merged = mergeAndDedupDraws(parts, lk);
  860 |   const closes = summarizeCloseHours(merged, lk);
  861 |   const errInfo =
  862 |     KING_FETCH_DEBUG && errors.length
  863 |       ? ` errors=${errors.length} ids=[${errors.map((x) => x.lotteryId).join(",")}]`
  864 |       : "";
  865 | 
  866 |   console.log(
  867 |     `[FETCH] ${lk} ${date} per-lottery=${lotteries.length} -> merged_draws=${
  868 |       merged.length
  869 |     } close_hours=[${closes.join(", ")}]${errInfo}`
  870 |   );
  871 |   return { success: true, data: merged };
  872 | }
  873 | 
  874 | /**
  875 |  * Monta prizes do draw (até 15), já filtrando vazios.
  876 |  */
  877 | function extractPrizes(draw) {
  878 |   const prizes = [];
  879 |   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
  880 |     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
  881 |     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
  882 |     prizes.push({ position: i, value: String(v) });
  883 |   }
  884 |   return prizes;
  885 | }

   24 | function normalizeLotteryKey(v, fallback = "PT_RIO") {
   25 |   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
   26 | 
   27 |   if (s === "RJ" || s === "RIO" || s === "PT-RIO" || s === "PT_RIO")
   28 |     return "PT_RIO";
   29 |   if (s === "FED" || s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
   30 | 
   31 |   return fallback;
   32 | }
   33 | 
   34 | /**
   35 |  * Hora robusta:
   36 |  * aceita "11:09", "11:09:00", "11h", "11hs", "11", "09-09", "09hs", etc.
   37 |  * retorna "HH" (2 dígitos) ou null
   38 |  */
   39 | function hourFromCloseHour(closeHour) {
   40 |   const s0 = String(closeHour ?? "").trim();
   41 |   if (!s0) return null;
   42 | 
   43 |   const m = s0.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
   44 |   if (!m) return null;
   45 | 
   46 |   const hh = Number(m[1]);
   47 |   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || hh < 0 || hh > 23) return null;
   48 | 
   49 |   return pad2(hh);
   50 | }

   65 | function safeReadJson(fp) {

   76 |   return !!(j && Array.isArray(j.missingHardByDay));
   77 | }

   79 | function hardCount(j) {
   80 |   return Array.isArray(j?.missingHardByDay) ? j.missingHardByDay.length : 0;
   81 | }
   82 | 
   83 | function inferLotteryFromJsonOrName(fp, j) {
   84 |   const byJson = String(j?.lottery || j?.loteria || j?.uf || "").trim();
   85 |   if (byJson) return normalizeLotteryKey(byJson, "");
   86 | 
   87 |   const name = path.basename(fp).toUpperCase();
   88 |   if (name.includes("PT_RIO") || name.includes("PT-RIO") || name.includes("RJ"))
   89 |     return "PT_RIO";
   90 |   if (name.includes("FEDERAL") || name.includes("FED")) return "FEDERAL";
   91 |   return "";
   92 | }
   93 | 
   94 | function walkFiles(dir, { maxFiles = 8000 } = {}) {
   95 |   const out = [];
   96 |   const stack = [dir];
   97 | 
   98 |   while (stack.length) {
   99 |     const cur = stack.pop();
  100 |     let entries = [];
  101 |     try {
  102 |       entries = fs.readdirSync(cur, { withFileTypes: true });
  103 |     } catch {
  104 |       continue;
  105 |     }
  106 | 
  107 |     for (const ent of entries) {
  108 |       const fp = path.join(cur, ent.name);
  109 |       if (ent.isDirectory()) {
  110 |         if (ent.name === "node_modules" || ent.name === ".git") continue;
  111 |         stack.push(fp);
  112 |       } else if (ent.isFile()) {
  113 |         out.push(fp);
  114 |         if (out.length >= maxFiles) return out;
  115 |       }
  116 |     }
  117 |   }
  118 | 
  119 |   return out;
  120 | }
  121 | 
  122 | /**
  123 |  * Auto-descobre o audit “melhor” PARA UMA LOTERIA:
  124 |  * - filtra por lottery (nome ou json)
  125 |  * - ordena por (hardCount desc, mtime desc)
  126 |  */
  127 | function findBestAuditFileForLottery(lottery) {
  128 |   const L = normalizeLotteryKey(lottery, "PT_RIO");
  129 | 
  130 |   const candidatesDirs = [
  131 |     path.join(process.cwd(), "backend", "data"),
  132 |     path.join(process.cwd(), "backend", "logs"),
  133 |     path.join(process.cwd(), "backend"),
  134 |   ];
  135 | 
  136 |   const candidates = [];
  137 | 
  138 |   for (const d of candidatesDirs) {
  139 |     if (!fs.existsSync(d)) continue;
  140 | 
  141 |     const files = walkFiles(d, { maxFiles: 8000 }).filter((fp) => {
  142 |       const name = path.basename(fp).toLowerCase();
  143 |       return name.endsWith(".json") && name.includes("audit");
  144 |     });
  145 | 
  146 |     for (const fp of files) {
  147 |       let st;
  148 |       try {
  149 |         st = fs.statSync(fp);
  150 |       } catch {
  151 |         continue;
  152 |       }
  153 | 
  154 |       const j = safeReadJson(fp);
  155 |       if (!looksLikeAuditJson(j)) continue;
  156 | 
  157 |       const lot = inferLotteryFromJsonOrName(fp, j);
  158 |       if (lot !== L) continue;
  159 | 
  160 |       candidates.push({ fp, mtimeMs: st.mtimeMs, hard: hardCount(j) });
  161 |     }
  162 |   }
  163 | 
  164 |   candidates.sort((a, b) => (b.hard - a.hard) || (b.mtimeMs - a.mtimeMs));
  165 |   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].fp : null;
  166 | }
  167 | 
  168 | async function getFetch() {
  169 |   if (typeof fetch === "function") return fetch;
  170 | 
  171 |   try {
  172 |     // eslint-disable-next-line global-require
  173 |     const mod = require("node-fetch");
  174 |     return mod.default || mod;
  175 |   } catch (e) {
  176 |     throw new Error(
  177 |       "Este Node não possui fetch global e o pacote 'node-fetch' não está instalado. " +
  178 |         "Use Node 18+ ou instale: npm i node-fetch"
  179 |     );
  180 |   }
  181 | }
  182 | 
  183 | /**
  184 |  * fetch JSON com timeout + retry
  185 |  * ENV:
  186 |  * - FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (default 15000)
  187 |  * - FETCH_RETRIES (default 2)
  188 |  * - FETCH_RETRY_DELAY_MS (default 600)
  189 |  */
  190 | async function fetchJson(url) {
  191 |   const _fetch = await getFetch();
  192 | 
  193 |   const TIMEOUT_MS = envInt("FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS", 15000);
  194 |   const RETRIES = envInt("FETCH_RETRIES", 2);
  195 |   const DELAY_MS = envInt("FETCH_RETRY_DELAY_MS", 600);
  196 | 
  197 |   const maxAttempts = 1 + Math.max(0, RETRIES);
  198 | 
  199 |   for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
  200 |     const ctrl =
  201 |       typeof AbortController !== "undefined" ? new AbortController() : null;
  202 |     const t = ctrl ? setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS) : null;
  203 | 
  204 |     try {
  205 |       const r = await _fetch(url, {
  206 |         headers: { accept: "application/json" },
  207 |         signal: ctrl ? ctrl.signal : undefined,
  208 |       });
  209 | 
  210 |       const txt = await r.text();
  211 |       let j = null;
  212 |       try {
  213 |         j = JSON.parse(txt);
  214 |       } catch {}
  215 | 
  216 |       if (!r.ok) {
  217 |         const msg = j && j.error ? j.error : txt || `HTTP ${r.status}`;
  218 |         throw new Error(msg);
  219 |       }
  220 | 
  221 |       // ✅ IMPORTANT: API pode responder 200 com ok:false
  222 |       if (j && j.ok === false) {

   52 |           data && typeof data === "object"
   53 |             ? JSON.stringify(data).slice(0, 250)
   54 |             : String(data || "");

   56 |         continue;
   57 |       }
   58 |       const ok = !!data?.ok && Array.isArray(data?.draws);
   59 |       const n = ok ? data.draws.length : -1;
   60 | 
   61 |       const closes = ok
   62 |         ? Array.from(
   63 |             new Set(
   64 |               data.draws
   65 |                 .map((d) => String(d?.close_hour || d?.closeHour || "").trim())
   66 |                 .filter(Boolean)
   67 |             )
   68 |           ).sort()
   69 |         : [];
   70 | 
   71 |       console.log(`[PROBE] id=${id} ok=${ok} draws=${n} close_hours=[${closes.join(", ")}]`);
   72 |     } catch (e) {
   73 |       const msg = e?.response?.status ? `HTTP_${e.response.status}` : (e?.code || e?.message || "ERR");

  150 |   } catch {
  151 |     // ignora
  152 |   }
  153 | 
  154 |   // determinístico
  155 |   return Array.from(candidates).sort((a, b) => String(a).localeCompare(String(b)));
  156 | }

  158 | function resolveCredentialsPath() {
  159 |   const rawEnv = String(process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS || "").trim();
  160 |   if (!rawEnv) return null;
  161 | 
  162 |   // remove aspas externas
  163 |   let p = rawEnv.replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, "").trim();
  164 | 
  165 |   // normaliza separadores
  166 |   p = path.normalize(p);
  167 | 
  168 |   // Se veio relativo, resolve a partir do repo (palpitaco),
  169 |   // mas também aceitamos relativo ao backend.
  170 |   if (!path.isAbsolute(p)) {
  171 |     const backendDir = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");
  172 |     const repoDir = path.resolve(backendDir, "..");
  173 | 
  174 |     const asBackend = path.resolve(backendDir, p);
  175 |     const asRepo = path.resolve(repoDir, p);
  176 | 
  177 |     if (fs.existsSync(asBackend)) {
  178 |       p = asBackend;
  179 |     } else {
  180 |       p = asRepo;
  181 |     }
  182 |   }
  183 | 
  184 |   if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
  185 |     const found = findCredentialCandidates();
  186 | 
  187 |     // Se achou exatamente 1, usa automaticamente (evita travar dev)
  188 |     if (found.length === 1) {
  189 |       const auto = found[0];
  190 |       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = auto;
  191 |       console.warn(
  192 |         "[WARN] GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS inválido. Usando automaticamente:",
  193 |         auto
  194 |       );
  195 |       return auto;
  196 |     }
  197 | 
  198 |     const hint =
  199 |       found.length === 0
  200 |         ? "Nenhum JSON candidato encontrado em backend/ ou _secrets."
  201 |         : "Encontrei mais de um candidato. Defina explicitamente no .env.local.";
  202 | 
  203 |     const list =
  204 |       found.length > 0 ? "\nCandidatos:\n- " + found.join("\n- ") : "";
  205 | 
  206 |     fail(
  207 |       `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS aponta para caminho inexistente:\n${p}\n\n` +
  208 |         `${hint}${list}\n\n` +
  209 |         `✅ Ajuste no .env.local, exemplo:\n` +
  210 |         `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=C:\\Users\\glaub\\palpitaco\\backend\\SEU_ARQUIVO.json`
  211 |     );
  212 |   }
  213 | 
  214 |   return p;
  215 | }

    1 | /**
    2 |  * Schedule oficial do PalPitaco (FRONT)
    3 |  * Fonte única de horários por loteria

    6 | 
    7 | function normalizeLotteryKey(v) {
    8 |   const s = String(v || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    9 | 
   10 |   if (s === "RJ") return "PT_RIO";
   11 |   if (s === "RIO") return "PT_RIO";
   12 |   if (s === "PT-RIO") return "PT_RIO";
   13 |   if (s === "PT_RIO") return "PT_RIO";
   14 | 
   15 |   if (s === "FED") return "FEDERAL";
   16 |   if (s === "BR") return "FEDERAL"; // compat defensiva
   17 |   if (s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
   18 | 
   19 |   return "PT_RIO"; // fallback seguro
   20 | }
   21 | 
   22 | function isValidHourFormat(h) {
   23 |   return /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(String(h || "").trim());
   24 | }
   25 | 
   26 | function sortHoursAsc(arr) {
   27 |   return [...arr].sort((a, b) => {
   28 |     const [ha, ma] = a.split(":").map(Number);
   29 |     const [hb, mb] = b.split(":").map(Number);
   30 |     return ha * 60 + ma - (hb * 60 + mb);
   31 |   });
   32 | }
   33 | 
   34 | export const SCHEDULES = Object.freeze({
   35 |   PT_RIO: Object.freeze([
   36 |     "09:00",
   37 |     "11:00",
   38 |     "14:00",
   39 |     "16:00",
   40 |     "18:00",
   41 |     "21:00",
   42 |   ]),
   43 |   FEDERAL: Object.freeze([
   44 |     "20:00",
   45 |   ]),
   46 | });
   47 | 
   48 | /**
   49 |  * Retorna os horários da loteria (normalizado e seguro)
   50 |  */
   51 | export function getScheduleByLottery(lottery = "PT_RIO") {
   52 |   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(lottery);
   53 | 
   54 |   const list = SCHEDULES[key];
   55 |   if (!Array.isArray(list)) return [];
   56 | 
   57 |   // Filtra formato inválido por segurança
   58 |   return sortHoursAsc(list.filter(isValidHourFormat));
   59 | }
   60 | 
   61 | /**
   62 |  * Retorna todos os horários conhecidos (flat + únicos + ordenados corretamente)
   63 |  */
   64 | export function getAllScheduleHours() {
   65 |   const all = Object.values(SCHEDULES)
   66 |     .flat()
   67 |     .filter(isValidHourFormat);
   68 | 
   69 |   return sortHoursAsc(Array.from(new Set(all)));
   70 | }
   71 | 
   72 | /**
   73 |  * Helper opcional:
   74 |  * Verifica se horário pertence à loteria
   75 |  */
   76 | export function isValidLotteryHour(lottery, hour) {
   77 |   const list = getScheduleByLottery(lottery);
   78 |   return list.includes(String(hour || "").trim());
   79 | }

   37 | function ymdToNumber(ymd) {
   38 |   if (!ymd) return NaN;
   39 |   const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   40 |   if (!m) return NaN;
   41 |   return Number(`${m[1]}${m[2]}${m[3]}`);
   42 | }
   43 | 
   44 | function dayDiffInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo) {
   45 |   const toUTC = (ymd) => {
   46 |     const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   47 |     if (!m) return null;
   48 |     return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   49 |   };
   50 | 
   51 |   const da = toUTC(ymdFrom);
   52 |   const db = toUTC(ymdTo);
   53 |   if (!da || !db) return NaN;
   54 | 
   55 |   const ms = db.getTime() - da.getTime();
   56 |   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return NaN;
   57 | 
   58 |   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000) + 1;
   59 | }
   60 | 
   61 | function normalizePositionsFromKey(positionsInputKey) {
   62 |   const key = String(positionsInputKey || "").trim();
   63 |   if (!key) return null;
   64 | 
   65 |   const nums = key
   66 |     .split(",")
   67 |     .map((x) => Number(String(x).trim()))
   68 |     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n));
   69 | 
   70 |   if (!nums.length) return null;
   71 |   return Array.from(new Set(nums)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
   72 | }
   73 | 
   74 | function normHHMM(value) {
   75 |   const s = String(value || "").trim();
   76 |   if (!s) return "";
   77 | 
   78 |   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   79 |   if (mh) return `${String(mh[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   80 | 
   81 |   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   82 |   if (mISO) {
   83 |     const hh = String(mISO[1]).padStart(2, "0");
   84 |     const mm = String(mISO[2]).padStart(2, "0");
   85 |     return `${hh}:${mm}`;
   86 |   }
   87 | 
   88 |   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   89 |   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   90 | 
   91 |   return s;
   92 | }
   93 | 
   94 | function toHourBucketLabel(closeHour) {

   96 |   if (!hhmm) return null;
   97 | 
   98 |   const hh = hhmm.slice(0, 2);
   99 |   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(hh)) return null;
  100 |   return `${hh}h`;
  101 | }

  225 | export function flattenDrawsForMilharV3(draws = []) {
  226 |   const rows = [];
  227 | 
  228 |   for (const draw of Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []) {
  229 |     const ymd = drawYmd(draw);
  230 |     const hour = drawHour(draw);
  231 |     const weekday = weekdayFromYmdV3(ymd);
  232 |     const timestamp = timestampFromParts(ymd, hour);
  233 | 
  234 |     const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes)
  235 |       ? draw.prizes
  236 |       : [];
  237 | 
  238 |     for (const prize of prizes) {
  239 |       const milhar = prizeMilhar(prize);
  240 | 
  241 |       if (!milhar) continue;
  242 | 
  243 |       rows.push({
  244 |         milhar,
  245 |         prefix: milhar.slice(0, 1),
  246 |         centena: milhar.slice(-3),
  247 |         dezena: milhar.slice(-2),
  248 |         position: prizePosition(prize),
  249 |         ymd,
  250 |         hour,
  251 |         weekday,
  252 |         timestamp,
  253 |       });
  254 |     }
  255 |   }
  256 | 
  257 |   return rows
  258 |     .filter((row) => Number.isFinite(row.timestamp))
  259 |     .sort((a, b) => {
  260 |       if (a.timestamp !== b.timestamp) {
  261 |         return a.timestamp - b.timestamp;
  262 |       }
  263 | 
  264 |       return a.position - b.position;
  265 |     })
  266 |     .map((row, index) => ({
  267 |       ...row,
  268 |       sequence: index + 1,
  269 |     }));
  270 | }

  272 | export function buildMilharCandidatesV3(centena) {
  273 |   const centena3 = normalizeCentena3V3(centena);
  274 | 
  275 |   if (!centena3) return [];
  276 | 
  277 |   return Array.from(
  278 |     { length: 10 },
  279 |     (_, prefix) => ({
  280 |       prefix: String(prefix),
  281 |       centena: centena3,
  282 |       milhar: `${prefix}${centena3}`,
  283 |     })
  284 |   );
  285 | }
  286 | 
  287 | function normalizeWeightsV3(weights = {}) {
  288 |   const merged = {
  289 |     ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3,
  290 |     ...(weights || {}),
  291 |   };
  292 | 
  293 |   const sanitized = {};
  294 | 
  295 |   for (const key of Object.keys(DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3)) {
  296 |     sanitized[key] = Math.max(
  297 |       0,
  298 |       safeNumber(merged[key], 0)
  299 |     );
  300 |   }
  301 | 
  302 |   const total = Object.values(sanitized)
  303 |     .reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0);
  304 | 
  305 |   if (total <= 0) {
  306 |     return {
  307 |       ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3,
  308 |     };
  309 |   }
  310 | 
  311 |   return Object.fromEntries(
  312 |     Object.entries(sanitized).map(
  313 |       ([key, value]) => [key, value / total]
  314 |     )
  315 |   );
  316 | }
  317 | 
  318 | function maxMapValue(map) {
  319 |   return Math.max(
  320 |     0,
  321 |     ...Array.from(map.values())
  322 |   );
  323 | }
  324 |

  326 |   if (!maximum || maximum <= 0) return 0;
  327 | 
  328 |   return Math.max(
  329 |     0,

  259 |           vA = Number(A.grupo || 0);
  260 |           vB = Number(B.grupo || 0);
  261 |           if (vA === vB) return 0;
  262 |           return (vA - vB) * dirMul;
  263 | 
  264 |         case "animal":

  266 |           vB = String(B.animalLabel || B.animalRaw || "");

  268 |             const c = cmpText(vA, vB) * dirMul;
  269 |             if (c !== 0) return c;
  270 |             return (Number(A.grupo || 0) - Number(B.grupo || 0)) * 1;
  271 |           }
  272 |

  281 |           }

  283 |     });

  244 | function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
  245 |   const d = safeStr(ymd);
  246 |   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
  247 | 
  248 |   let out = d;
  249 |   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
  250 |   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
  251 | 
  252 |   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
  253 |   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
  254 | 
  255 |   return out;
  256 | }

  258 | function normalizeSingleDateWithBounds(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd) {
  259 |   const d = clampYmd(dateIn, minYmd, maxYmd);
  260 |   if (d) return d;
  261 | 
  262 |   const fallback =
  263 |     clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minYmd, maxYmd) ||
  264 |     (isYMD(maxYmd) ? maxYmd : null) ||
  265 |     (isYMD(minYmd) ? minYmd : null) ||
  266 |     todayYMDLocal();
  267 | 
  268 |   return fallback;
  269 | }

  274 | function normalizeBoundsResponse(b) {

  278 |   const maxYmd = isYMD(maxRaw) ? maxRaw : null;
  279 |   return { minYmd, maxYmd, source: safeStr(b?.source || "") };

   27 | function drawTs(draw) {
   28 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   29 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   30 |   if (!y || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   31 | 
   32 |   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
   33 |   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   34 |   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   35 |   const add = mins >= 0 ? mins * 60 * 1000 : 0;
   36 |   return base + add;
   37 | }
   38 | 
   39 | function drawKey(draw) {
   40 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   41 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   42 |   if (!y || !h) return "";
   43 |   return `${y}|${h}`;
   44 | }
   45 | 
   46 | function dedupeAndSort(draws) {
   47 |   const map = new Map();
   48 | 
   49 |   for (const d of Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []) {
   50 |     const key = drawKey(d);
   51 |     if (!key) continue;
   52 |     if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, d);
   53 |   }
   54 | 
   55 |   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
   56 | }
   57 | 
   58 | function top5Groups(draw) {
   59 |   const out = [];
   60 | 
   61 |   for (const p of Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : []) {
   62 |     const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
   63 |     const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(p));
   64 | 
   65 |     if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 5) continue;
   66 |     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
   67 | 
   68 |     out.push({ pos, grupo: g });
   69 |   }
   70 | 
   71 |   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
   72 | }
   73 | 
   74 | function ensureRow(map, key) {
   75 |   if (!map.has(key)) {
   76 |     map.set(key, { total: 0, hitTop5: 0, hitFirst: 0 });
   77 |   }
   78 | 
   79 |   return map.get(key);
   80 | }

   83 |   return d ? ((n / d) * 100).toFixed(2) : "0.00";
   84 | }

   86 | function assertComputedSlotMatchesNextDraw({ computed, found, nextSlot, drawLast }) {

   88 |   const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");

  106 | function assertPredictionIntegrity({ computed, drawLast, nextDraw }) {
  107 |   const top = Array.isArray(computed?.top) ? computed.top.slice(0, 3) : [];
  108 | 
  109 |   if (!top.length) {
  110 |     throw new Error(
  111 |       [
  112 |         "TOP3 vazio para caso com próximo sorteio real.",
  113 |         `Último sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(drawLast)} ${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast))}`,
  114 |         `Próximo sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(nextDraw)} ${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(nextDraw))}`,
  115 |         `Scenario: ${computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE"}`,
  116 |       ].join("\n")
  117 |     );
  118 |   }
  119 | 
  120 |   const seen = new Set();
  121 | 
  122 |   for (const item of top) {
  123 |     const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
  124 | 
  125 |     if (!Number.isFinite(grupo) || grupo < 1 || grupo > 25) {
  126 |       throw new Error(`Grupo inválido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
  127 |     }
  128 | 
  129 |     if (seen.has(grupo)) {
  130 |       throw new Error(`Grupo repetido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(top)}`);
  131 |     }
  132 | 
  133 |     seen.add(grupo);
  134 | 
  135 |     const confidence = Number(
  136 |       item?.displayConfidence ??
  137 |         item?.confidence ??
  138 |         item?.scoreProb ??
  139 |         item?.prob ??
  140 |         0
  141 |     );
  142 | 
  143 |     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence)) {
  144 |       throw new Error(`Confiança inválida no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
  145 |     }
  146 | 
  147 |     if (confidence < 0) {
  148 |       throw new Error(`Confiança negativa no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
  149 |     }
  150 |   }
  151 | }

   95 |     found?.hour || found?.slot?.hour || nextSlot?.hour || ""
   96 |   );
   97 | 
   98 |   if (computedNextYmd !== expectedYmd || computedNextHour !== expectedHour) {
   99 |     throw new Error(
  100 |       [
  101 |         "TOP3 calculou slot diferente do próximo sorteio real.",
  102 |         `Último sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(drawLast)} ${toHourBucket(
  103 |           pickDrawHour(drawLast)
  104 |         )}`,
  105 |         `Esperado: ${expectedYmd} ${expectedHour}`,
  106 |         `Calculado: ${computedNextYmd} ${computedNextHour}`,
  107 |         `Scenario: ${computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE"}`,
  108 |       ].join("\n")
  109 |     );
  110 |   }
  111 | }
  112 | 
  113 | function assertPredictionIntegrity({ computed, drawLast, nextDraw }) {
  114 |   const top = Array.isArray(computed?.top) ? computed.top.slice(0, 3) : [];
  115 | 
  116 |   if (!top.length) {
  117 |     throw new Error(
  118 |       [
  119 |         "TOP3 vazio para caso com próximo sorteio real.",
  120 |         `Último sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(drawLast)} ${toHourBucket(
  121 |           pickDrawHour(drawLast)
  122 |         )}`,
  123 |         `Próximo sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(nextDraw)} ${toHourBucket(
  124 |           pickDrawHour(nextDraw)
  125 |         )}`,
  126 |         `Scenario: ${computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE"}`,
  127 |       ].join("\n")
  128 |     );
  129 |   }
  130 | 
  131 |   const seen = new Set();
  132 | 
  133 |   for (const item of top) {
  134 |     const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
  135 | 
  136 |     if (!Number.isFinite(grupo) || grupo < 1 || grupo > 25) {
  137 |       throw new Error(`Grupo inválido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
  138 |     }
  139 | 
  140 |     if (seen.has(grupo)) {
  141 |       throw new Error(`Grupo repetido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(top)}`);
  142 |     }
  143 | 
  144 |     seen.add(grupo);
  145 | 
  146 |     const confidence = Number(
  147 |       item?.displayConfidence ??
  148 |         item?.confidence ??
  149 |         item?.scoreProb ??
  150 |         item?.prob ??
  151 |         0
  152 |     );
  153 | 
  154 |     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence)) {
  155 |       throw new Error(`Confiança inválida no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
  156 |     }
  157 | 
  158 |     if (confidence < 0) {
  159 |       throw new Error(`Confiança negativa no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
  160 |     }
  161 |   }
  162 | }
  163 |

   53 | function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
   54 |   const collected = collectEvidence({
   55 |     item,
   56 |     context,
   57 |     config: scoreConfig,
   58 |   });
   59 | 
   60 |   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
   61 | 
   62 |   return {
   63 |     ...item,
   64 |     score: strength.score,
   65 |     confidence: strength.confidence,
   66 |     reasons: strength.reasons,
   67 |     signals: strength.signals,
   68 |     evidenceCount: collected.count,
   69 |     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
   70 |   };
   71 | }
   72 | 
   73 | function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
   74 | 
   75 |   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
   76 |     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
   77 |     .sort((a, b) => {
   78 | 
   79 |       if (b.score !== a.score)
   80 |         return b.score - a.score;
   81 | 
   82 |       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
   83 |         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
   84 | 
   85 |       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
   86 | 
   87 |     });
   88 | 
   89 | }
   90 |

   92 | scoreItem,

   31 |   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));

   53 | function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
   54 |   const collected = collectEvidence({
   55 |     item,
   56 |     context,
   57 |     config: scoreConfig,
   58 |   });
   59 | 
   60 |   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
   61 | 
   62 |   const probability = Math.max(
   63 |     0,
   64 |     Math.min(
   65 |       100,
   66 |       Number(item.scoreProb || item.rawScoreProb || 0) * 100
   67 |     )
   68 |   );
   69 | 
   70 |   const finalScore = Math.round(
   71 |     (strength.score * 0.45) +
   72 |     (probability * 0.55)
   73 |   );
   74 | 
   75 |   const finalConfidence = Math.round(
   76 |     (strength.confidence * 0.40) +
   77 |     (probability * 0.60)
   78 |   );
   79 | 
   80 |   return {
   81 |     ...item,
   82 | 
   83 |     score: finalScore,
   84 | 
   85 |     confidence: finalConfidence,
   86 | 
   87 |     reasons: [
   88 |       ...strength.reasons,
   89 |       `Probabilidade V3: ${probability.toFixed(2)}%`,
   90 |     ],
   91 | 
   92 |     signals: {
   93 |       ...strength.signals,
   94 |       probability,
   95 |     },
   96 | 
   97 |     evidenceCount: collected.count,
   98 | 
   99 |     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
  100 |   };
  101 | }
  102 | 
  103 | function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {

  112 |       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
  113 |         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
  114 |

    1 | import { isYMD, toHourBucket } from "../top3.formatters";

   16 | function emptyAnalytics() {
   17 |   return { top: [], meta: null };
   18 | }
   19 | 
   20 | function safeCacheRef(ref) {
   21 |   if (!ref || typeof ref !== "object") {
   22 |     return { current: { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() } };
   23 |   }
   24 | 
   25 |   if (!ref.current || typeof ref.current !== "object") {
   26 |     ref.current = { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() };
   27 |   }
   28 | 
   29 |   return ref;
   30 | }
