   32 | function sameSlot(meta, targetYmd, targetHour) {
   33 |   const targetY = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
   34 |   const targetH = toHourBucket(targetHour || "");
   35 | 
   36 |   const y = String(meta?.next?.ymd || "").trim();
   37 |   const h = toHourBucket(meta?.next?.hour || "");
   38 | 
   39 |   return isYMD(targetY) && !!targetH && y === targetY && h === targetH;
   40 | }
   41 | 
   42 | function firstFiniteNumber(...values) {
   43 |   for (const v of values) {
   44 |     const n = Number(v);
   45 |     if (Number.isFinite(n)) return n;
   46 |   }
   47 | 
   48 |   return NaN;
   49 | }
   50 | 
   51 | function sanitizeTop3(top) {
   52 |   const out = [];
   53 |   const seen = new Set();
   54 | 
   55 |   for (const item of Array.isArray(top) ? top : []) {
   56 |     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
   57 | 
   58 |     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
   59 | 
   60 |     const grupo = Math.trunc(g);
   61 |     if (seen.has(grupo)) continue;
   62 | 
   63 |     const confidence = firstFiniteNumber(
   64 |       item?.displayConfidence,
   65 |       item?.confidence,
   66 |       item?.scoreProb,
   67 |       item?.prob,
   68 |       0
   69 |     );
   70 | 
   71 |     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence) || confidence < 0) continue;
   72 | 
   73 |     seen.add(grupo);
   74 | 
   75 |     out.push({
   76 |       ...item,
   77 |       grupo,
   78 |       displayConfidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.displayConfidence))
   79 |         ? Number(item.displayConfidence)
   80 |         : confidence,
   81 |       confidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.confidence))
   82 |         ? Number(item.confidence)
   83 |         : confidence,
   84 |       scoreProb: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.scoreProb))
   85 |         ? Number(item.scoreProb)
   86 |         : confidence,
   87 |     });
   88 | 
   89 |     if (out.length >= 3) break;
   90 |   }
   91 | 
   92 |   return out;
   93 | }
   94 | 
   95 | export function computeTop3Analytics({
   96 |   rangeDraws,
   97 |   baseDrawState,
   98 |   analyticsCacheRef,
   99 |   lotteryKeySafe,
  100 |   lookback,
  101 |   rangeInfo,
  102 |   todayDraws,
  103 |   sanitizeHistoricalDraws,
  104 |   targetYmd = "",
  105 |   targetHourBucket = "",
  106 | }) {
  107 |   const cacheRef = safeCacheRef(analyticsCacheRef);
  108 |   const lotteryKey = String(lotteryKeySafe || "").trim();
  109 | 
  110 |   const rawList = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
  111 |   const drawLast = baseDrawState || null;
  112 | 
  113 |   if (!rawList.length || !drawLast || !lotteryKey) {
  114 |     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
  115 |     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };
  116 |     return empty;
  117 |   }
  118 | 
  119 |   const lastG = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(drawLast));
  120 |   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || "";
  121 |   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast)) || "";
  122 | 
  123 |   const forcedTargetY = isYMD(targetYmd) ? targetYmd : "";
  124 |   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
  125 | 
  126 |   if (
  127 |     !Number.isFinite(lastG) ||
  128 |     lastG < 1 ||
  129 |     lastG > 25 ||
  130 |     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  131 |     !lastH ||
  132 |     !isYMD(forcedTargetY) ||
  133 |     !forcedTargetH
  134 |   ) {
  135 |     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
  136 |     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };
  137 |     return empty;
  138 |   }
  139 | 
  140 |   const historicalList =
  141 |     typeof sanitizeHistoricalDraws === "function"
  142 |       ? sanitizeHistoricalDraws({
  143 |           draws: rawList,
  144 |           lotteryKey,
  145 |           baseDraw: drawLast,
  146 |         })
  147 |       : rawList;
  148 | 
  149 |   const safeHistoricalList = Array.isArray(historicalList)
  150 |     ? historicalList
  151 |     : [];
  152 | 
  153 |   if (!safeHistoricalList.length) {
  154 |     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
  155 |     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };
  156 |     return empty;
  157 |   }
  158 | 
  159 |   const firstDraw = safeHistoricalList[0] || null;
  160 |   const lastDrawInRange =
  161 |     safeHistoricalList[safeHistoricalList.length - 1] || null;
  162 | 
  163 |   const todaySignature = (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [])
  164 |     .map((d) => `${pickDrawYMD(d) || ""}@${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)) || ""}`)
  165 |     .filter(Boolean)
  166 |     .join(",");
  167 | 
  168 |   const cacheKey = [
  169 |     "V3",
  170 |     lotteryKey,
  171 |     lookback,
  172 |     rangeInfo?.from || "",
  173 |     rangeInfo?.to || "",
  174 |     safeHistoricalList.length,
  175 |     rawList.length,
  176 |     todaySignature,
  177 |     firstDraw ? pickDrawYMD(firstDraw) || "" : "",
  178 |     firstDraw ? toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(firstDraw)) || "" : "",
  179 |     lastDrawInRange ? pickDrawYMD(lastDrawInRange) || "" : "",
  180 |     lastDrawInRange ? toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(lastDrawInRange)) || "" : "",
  181 |     lastY,
  182 |     lastH,
  183 |     Math.trunc(lastG),
  184 |     forcedTargetY,
  185 |     forcedTargetH,
  186 |   ].join("|");
  187 | 
  188 |   if (cacheRef.current?.key === cacheKey) {
  189 |     return cacheRef.current.value || emptyAnalytics();
  190 |   }
  191 | 
  192 |   const computed =
  193 |     computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  194 |       lotteryKey,
  195 |       drawsRange: safeHistoricalList,
  196 |       drawLast,
  197 |       drawsToday: Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [],
  198 |       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  199 |       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  200 |       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  201 |       topN: 3,
  202 |       targetYmdOverride: forcedTargetY,
  203 |       targetHourOverride: forcedTargetH,
  204 |     }) || emptyAnalytics();
  205 | 
  206 |   if (!sameSlot(computed?.meta, forcedTargetY, forcedTargetH)) {
  207 |     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
  208 |     cacheRef.current = { key: cacheKey, value: empty };
  209 |     return empty;
  210 |   }
  211 | 
  212 |   const value = {
  213 |     ...computed,
  214 |     top: sanitizeTop3(computed?.top),
  215 |   };
  216 | 
  217 |   cacheRef.current = { key: cacheKey, value };
  218 |   return value;
  219 | }

   55 | function dedupeAndSortDraws(draws) {
   56 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   57 |   const map = new Map();
   58 | 
   59 |   for (const d of list) {
   60 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   61 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   62 |     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
   63 | 
   64 |     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
   65 | 
   66 |     const key =
   67 |       String(d?.id || d?.drawId || "").trim() ||
   68 |       (isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "");
   69 | 
   70 |     if (!key) continue;
   71 | 
   72 |     const prev = map.get(key);
   73 | 
   74 |     if (!prev || ts < drawSortTs(prev)) {
   75 |       map.set(key, d);
   76 |     }
   77 |   }
   78 | 
   79 |   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawSortTs(a) - drawSortTs(b));
   80 | }
   81 | 
   82 | function makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour) {
   83 |   const from = toHourBucket(fromHour);
   84 |   const to = toHourBucket(toHour);
   85 | 
   86 |   if (!from || !to) return "";
   87 | 
   88 |   return `${from}->${to}`;
   89 | }

   91 | function getNestedMap(map, key) {
   92 |   if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, new Map());
   93 |   return map.get(key);
   94 | }

   96 | function getHistoryBeforeTarget(draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket) {
   97 |   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
   98 |   const target = slotTs(targetYmd, targetHourBucket);
   99 | 
  100 |   if (!list.length || !Number.isFinite(target)) return [];
  101 | 
  102 |   return list.filter((d) => {
  103 |     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
  104 |     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < target;

  108 | export function computeChainTransitions(draws) {
  109 |   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
  110 |   const transitionsAny = new Map();
  111 |   const transitionsByEdge = new Map();
  112 | 
  113 |   for (let i = 0; i < list.length - 1; i += 1) {
  114 |     const fromDraw = list[i];
  115 |     const toDraw = list[i + 1];
  116 | 
  117 |     const fromGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(fromDraw));
  118 |     const toGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(toDraw));
  119 |     const fromHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(fromDraw));
  120 |     const toHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(toDraw));
  121 | 
  122 |     if (!Number.isFinite(fromGrupo) || !Number.isFinite(toGrupo)) continue;
  123 |     if (fromGrupo < 1 || fromGrupo > 25) continue;
  124 |     if (toGrupo < 1 || toGrupo > 25) continue;
  125 |     if (!fromHour || !toHour) continue;
  126 | 
  127 |     const anyRow = getNestedMap(transitionsAny, fromGrupo);
  128 |     anyRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(anyRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
  129 | 
  130 |     const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour);
  131 |     if (!edgeKey) continue;
  132 | 
  133 |     const edgeMap = getNestedMap(transitionsByEdge, edgeKey);
  134 |     const edgeRow = getNestedMap(edgeMap, fromGrupo);
  135 |     edgeRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(edgeRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
  136 |   }
  137 | 
  138 |   return { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge };
  139 | }
  140 | 
  141 | function findPreviousDrawForTarget(draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket) {
  142 |   const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
  143 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  144 | 
  145 |   const target = slotTs(targetYmd, targetHourBucket);
  146 |   if (!Number.isFinite(target)) return null;
  147 | 
  148 |   let prev = null;
  149 | 
  150 |   for (const d of list) {
  151 |     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
  152 |     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
  153 | 
  154 |     if (ts < target) {
  155 |       prev = d;
  156 |     } else {
  157 |       break;
  158 |     }
  159 |   }
  160 | 
  161 |   return prev;
  162 | }
  163 | 
  164 | function rankTransitionMap(rowMap, basedOnGrupo, topN) {
  165 |   if (!rowMap || !rowMap.size) return [];
  166 | 
  167 |   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
  168 |     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
  169 |     0
  170 |   );
  171 | 
  172 |   if (total <= 0) return [];
  173 | 
  174 |   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
  175 |     .sort((a, b) => {
  176 |       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
  177 |       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
  178 |     })
  179 |     .slice(0, Math.max(1, safeInt(topN, 3)))
  180 |     .map(([grupo, freq], i) => {
  181 |       const prob = Number(freq) / total;
  182 | 
  183 |       return {
  184 |         rank: i + 1,
  185 |         grupo: Number(grupo),
  186 |         freq: Number(freq),
  187 |         prob,
  188 |         probPct: Number((prob * 100).toFixed(2)),
  189 |         basedOnGrupo: Number(basedOnGrupo),
  190 |         totalTransitionsFromGrupo: total,
  191 |       };
  192 |     });
  193 | }

  218 |       basedOnYmd: pickDrawYMD(lastDraw),

   68 | function drawQualityScore(draw) {
   69 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   70 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   71 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   72 | 
   73 |   let score = 0;
   74 |   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
   75 |   if (h) score += 20;
   76 |   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
   77 | 
   78 |   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
   79 |   if (hasP1) score += 15;
   80 | 
   81 |   return score;
   82 | }

   91 |         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
   92 |           ? Number(p.colocacao)
   93 |           : null;
   94 | 
   95 |   return pos;
   96 | }

  253 |   return new Set((Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : []).map(toHourBucket));

  261 | export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
  262 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  263 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  264 | 
  265 |   const sorted = [...list]
  266 |     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  267 |     .sort((a, b) => {
  268 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  269 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  270 |       return tb - ta;
  271 |     });
  272 | 
  273 |   return sorted[0] || null;
  274 | }

  280 | ) {

  282 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  283 | 
  284 |   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
  285 |   if (t < 0) return null;

  288 |     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))

  291 |     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
  292 | 
  293 |   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;

  548 | export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
  549 |   const map = new Map();
  550 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  551 | 
  552 |   for (const d of list) {
  553 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  554 |     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
  555 |     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
  556 | 
  557 |     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  558 | 
  559 |     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
  560 |     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
  561 | 
  562 |     if (!prev) {
  563 |       map.set(drawKey, d);
  564 |       continue;
  565 |     }
  566 | 
  567 |     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
  568 |       map.set(drawKey, d);
  569 |     }
  570 |   }
  571 | 
  572 |   return map;

  670 | function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  671 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  672 |   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
  673 |   const out = new Map();
  674 | 
  675 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  676 |     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  677 |     out.set(g, n / denom);
  678 |   }
  679 | 
  680 |   return out;
  681 | }

  683 | function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
  684 |   draws,
  685 |   lotteryKey,
  686 |   targetHour,
  687 |   targetDow,
  688 |   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  689 | ) {
  690 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  691 |   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  692 |   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  693 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  694 | 
  695 |   const freq = new Map();
  696 |   let totalSamples = 0;
  697 | 
  698 |   for (const d of list) {
  699 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  700 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  701 |     if (!h || !y) continue;
  702 | 
  703 |     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  704 |       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
  705 |       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
  706 |     } else {
  707 |       if (h !== target) continue;
  708 |     }
  709 | 
  710 |     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  711 |     if (!items.length) continue;
  712 | 
  713 |     totalSamples += 1;
  714 | 
  715 |     for (const it of items) {
  716 |       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  717 |       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  718 |       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  719 |     }
  720 |   }
  721 | 
  722 |   const out = new Map();
  723 |   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
  724 | 
  725 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  726 |     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  727 |     out.set(g, n / denom);
  728 |   }
  729 | 
  730 |   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
  731 | }

  737 | function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
  738 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  739 |   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
  740 |     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
  741 |     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
  742 |     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
  743 |     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
  744 |     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
  745 |     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
  746 |     return tsa - tsb;
  747 |   });
  748 | }

  750 | function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
  751 |   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
  752 |   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  753 |   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  754 |   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
  755 | 
  756 |   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
  757 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  758 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  759 |     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  760 |     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
  761 |   });
  762 | 
  763 |   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
  764 | }

 1235 |     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));

 2845 | function emptyGroupMap() {
 2846 |   const out = new Map();
 2847 |   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
 2848 |   return out;

 2851 | function getTop5GroupSet(draw) {
 2852 |   return new Set(
 2853 |     getAllTop5Groups(draw)
 2854 |       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
 2855 |       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)

 2859 | function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
 2860 |   const out = new Map();
 2861 |   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
 2862 |   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
 2863 |   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
 2864 |   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
 2865 | 
 2866 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2867 |     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
 2868 |     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
 2869 |   }
 2870 | 
 2871 |   return out;
 2872 | }
 2873 | 
 2874 | function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
 2875 |   const n = Number(samples || 0);
 2876 |   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
 2877 |   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
 2878 | }

 3243 | function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
 3244 |   const candidates = [
 3245 |     prize?.milhar,
 3246 |     prize?.milhar4,
 3247 |     prize?.numero,
 3248 |     prize?.number,
 3249 |     prize?.value,
 3250 |     prize?.result,
 3251 |     prize?.resultado,
 3252 |     prize?.premio,
 3253 |   ];
 3254 | 
 3255 |   for (const v of candidates) {
 3256 |     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
 3257 |     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
 3258 |   }
 3259 | 
 3260 |   return "";
 3261 | }
 3262 | 
 3263 | function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
 3264 |   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3265 |   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
 3266 | 
 3267 |   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
 3268 |   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3269 | 
 3270 |   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3271 |   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3272 | 
 3273 |   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
 3274 | }

 3276 | function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
 3277 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 3278 | 
 3279 |   const rows = prizes
 3280 |     .map((p) => {
 3281 |       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
 3282 |       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
 3283 |       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
 3284 | 
 3285 |       return {
 3286 |         pos,
 3287 |         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
 3288 |         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
 3289 |         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
 3290 |         grupo,
 3291 |       };
 3292 |     })
 3293 |     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
 3294 |     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
 3295 | 
 3296 |   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
 3297 |   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
 3298 |   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
 3299 |   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
 3300 | 
 3301 |   const countMap = (arr) => {
 3302 |     const m = new Map();
 3303 |     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
 3304 |     return Array.from(m.entries())
 3305 |       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
 3306 |       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
 3307 |   };
 3308 | 
 3309 |   return {
 3310 |     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
 3311 |     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
 3312 |     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
 3313 |     grupos,
 3314 |     dezenas,
 3315 |     centenas,
 3316 |     milhares,
 3317 |     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
 3318 |     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
 3319 |     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
 3320 |     rows,
 3321 |     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
 3322 |   };
 3323 | }

 3327 | function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
 3328 |   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
 3329 | 
 3330 |   let score = 0;
 3331 |   const details = {};
 3332 | 
 3333 |   const add = (key, value) => {
 3334 |     if (!value) return;
 3335 |     score += value;
 3336 |     details[key] = value;
 3337 |   };
 3338 | 
 3339 |   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
 3340 |   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
 3341 | 
 3342 |   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
 3343 |   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
 3344 | 
 3345 |   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
 3346 |   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
 3347 | 
 3348 |   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
 3349 |     add("firstGrupo",45);
 3350 | 
 3351 |   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
 3352 |     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
 3353 |       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
 3354 |   }
 3355 | 
 3356 |   add(
 3357 |     "groupOverlap",
 3358 |     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
 3359 |   );
 3360 | 
 3361 |   add(
 3362 |     "dezenaOverlap",
 3363 |     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
 3364 |   );
 3365 | 
 3366 |   add(
 3367 |     "centenaOverlap",
 3368 |     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
 3369 |   );
 3370 | 
 3371 |   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3372 |   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3373 | 
 3374 |   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
 3375 |     add("repeatExists",8);
 3376 | 
 3377 |   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
 3378 |     add("repeatEqual",6);
 3379 | 
 3380 |   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
 3381 |     add("signature",60);
 3382 | 
 3383 |   if (a.hour === b.hour)
 3384 |     add("hour",8);
 3385 | 
 3386 |   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
 3387 | }

 3392 | function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
 3393 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 3394 |   if (!currentScene) return [];
 3395 | 
 3396 |   const ordered = list
 3397 |     .map((draw) => ({
 3398 |       draw,
 3399 |       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
 3400 |     }))
 3401 |     .filter((x) => x.scene)
 3402 |     .sort((a, b) => {
 3403 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3404 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3405 |       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
 3406 |     });
 3407 | 
 3408 |   return ordered
 3409 |     .map((item, idx) => {
 3410 |       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
 3411 |       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
 3412 | 
 3413 |       return {
 3414 |         draw: item.draw,
 3415 |         scene: item.scene,
 3416 |         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
 3417 |         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
 3418 |         score,
 3419 |       };
 3420 |     })
 3421 |     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
 3422 |     .sort((a, b) => {
 3423 |       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
 3424 |         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3425 |       }
 3426 | 
 3427 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3428 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3429 |       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
 3430 |     })
 3431 |     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
 3432 | }

 3436 | 
 3437 | function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
 3438 |   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
 3439 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 3440 | 
 3441 |   const freq = new Map();
 3442 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
 3443 | 
 3444 |   let totalWeight = 0;
 3445 | 
 3446 |   for (const item of ranking) {
 3447 |     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
 3448 |     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
 3449 | 
 3450 |     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
 3451 |     if (score <= 0) continue;
 3452 | 
 3453 |     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
 3454 |     totalWeight += score;
 3455 |   }
 3456 | 
 3457 |   const prob = new Map();
 3458 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 3459 |     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
 3460 |   }
 3461 | 
 3462 |   return {
 3463 |     freq,
 3464 |     prob,
 3465 |     samples: ranking.length,
 3466 |     totalWeight,
 3467 |   };
 3468 | }

 3866 |   function pct(n, d) {
 3867 |     if (!d) return 0;
 3868 |     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
 3869 |   }

 3871 |   function groupBy(keyFn) {
 3872 |     const map = new Map();
 3873 | 
 3874 |     for (const slot of validated) {
 3875 |       const key = keyFn(slot);
 3876 |       const current = map.get(key) || {
 3877 |         key,
 3878 |         total: 0,
 3879 |         top1Hits: 0,
 3880 |         top3Hits: 0,
 3881 |       };
 3882 | 
 3883 |       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
 3884 |       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
 3885 |       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
 3886 | 
 3887 |       current.total += 1;
 3888 |       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
 3889 |       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
 3890 | 
 3891 |       map.set(key, current);
 3892 |     }
 3893 | 
 3894 |     return Array.from(map.values())
 3895 |       .map((x) => ({
 3896 |         ...x,
 3897 |         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
 3898 |         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
 3899 |       }))
 3900 |       .sort((a, b) => {
 3901 |         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
 3902 |         return b.total - a.total;
 3903 |       });
 3904 |   }
 3905 | 
 3906 |   return {
 3907 |     lotteryKey,
 3908 |     total,
 3909 |     top1Hits,
 3910 |     top3Hits,
 3911 |     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
 3912 |     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
 3913 |     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
 3914 |     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
 3915 |     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
 3916 |       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
 3917 |       hour: slot.targetHour,
 3918 |       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
 3919 |       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
 3920 |       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
 3921 |       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3922 |         .slice(0, 3)
 3923 |         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
 3924 |       top1Hit:
 3925 |         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
 3926 |       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3927 |         .slice(0, 3)
 3928 |         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
 3929 |     })),
 3930 |   };

 4074 |         status: "pending",
 4075 |       });
 4076 |       continue;
 4077 |     }
 4078 | 
 4079 |     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
 4080 |       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
 4081 |       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
 4082 |     );
 4083 | 
 4084 |     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
 4085 |       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
 4086 |         pickDrawYMD(d),
 4087 |         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
 4088 |       );
 4089 |       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
 4090 |     });
 4091 |

 4093 |       .filter((d) => {
 4094 |         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
 4095 |           pickDrawYMD(d),
 4096 |           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
 4097 |         );

 4102 |         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4103 |         return ta - tb;
 4104 |       });
 4105 | 
 4106 |     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
 4107 |       lotteryKey,
 4108 |       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
 4109 |       drawLast: baseDraw,

 4114 |       topN: 3,

 4116 |       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
 4117 |     });

 4119 | 
 4120 |     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
 4121 |     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");

 4126 |       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
 4127 |         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
 4128 |         : [];

 4131 | 
 4132 |     const hit =
 4133 |       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
 4134 |         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
 4135 |         : null;
 4136 | 
 4137 |     timeline.push({
 4138 |       targetYmd,
 4139 |       targetHour: slotHour,
 4140 |       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
 4141 |       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
 4142 |       top3,
 4143 |       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
 4144 |       hit,
 4145 |       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
 4146 |     });
 4147 | 
 4148 |     if (currentDraw) {
 4149 |       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
 4150 |     } else {
 4151 |       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
 4152 |     }
 4153 |   }
 4154 | 
 4155 |   return timeline;
 4156 |

   32 | function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
   33 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   34 |   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
   35 |   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
   36 | 
   37 |   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
   38 | 
   39 |   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
   40 | 
   41 |   return list
   42 |     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
   43 |     .map((draw) => {
   44 |       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   45 |       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   46 |       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
   47 |     })
   48 |     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
   49 |     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;

   56 | function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
   57 |   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
   58 |   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
   59 | 
   60 |   const n = Number(s);
   61 |   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
   62 |   if (n === 0) return 25;
   63 | 
   64 |   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
   65 |   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
   66 | }
   67 | 
   68 | function drawQualityScore(draw) {
   69 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   70 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   71 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   72 | 
   73 |   let score = 0;
   74 |   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
   75 |   if (h) score += 20;
   76 |   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
   77 | 
   78 |   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
   79 |   if (hasP1) score += 15;
   80 | 
   81 |   return score;
   82 | }

   84 | export function guessPrizePos(p) {
   85 |   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
   86 |     ? Number(p.position)
   87 |     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
   88 |       ? Number(p.posicao)
   89 |       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
   90 |         ? Number(p.pos)
   91 |         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
   92 |           ? Number(p.colocacao)
   93 |           : null;
   94 | 
   95 |   return pos;
   96 | }

  261 | export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
  262 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  263 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  264 | 
  265 |   const sorted = [...list]
  266 |     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  267 |     .sort((a, b) => {
  268 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  269 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  270 |       return tb - ta;
  271 |     });
  272 | 
  273 |   return sorted[0] || null;
  274 | }

  276 | export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
  277 |   draws,
  278 |   targetHourBucket,
  279 |   schedule
  280 | ) {
  281 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  282 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  283 | 
  284 |   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
  285 |   if (t < 0) return null;
  286 | 
  287 |   const candidates = list
  288 |     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  289 |     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
  290 |     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
  291 |     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
  292 | 
  293 |   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
  294 | }

  548 | export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {

  670 | function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  671 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  672 |   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
  673 |   const out = new Map();
  674 | 
  675 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  676 |     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  677 |     out.set(g, n / denom);
  678 |   }
  679 | 
  680 |   return out;
  681 | }

  683 | function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
  684 |   draws,
  685 |   lotteryKey,
  686 |   targetHour,
  687 |   targetDow,
  688 |   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  689 | ) {
  690 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  691 |   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  692 |   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  693 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  694 | 
  695 |   const freq = new Map();
  696 |   let totalSamples = 0;
  697 | 
  698 |   for (const d of list) {
  699 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  700 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  701 |     if (!h || !y) continue;
  702 | 
  703 |     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  704 |       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
  705 |       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
  706 |     } else {
  707 |       if (h !== target) continue;
  708 |     }
  709 | 
  710 |     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  711 |     if (!items.length) continue;
  712 | 
  713 |     totalSamples += 1;
  714 | 
  715 |     for (const it of items) {
  716 |       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  717 |       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  718 |       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  719 |     }
  720 |   }
  721 | 
  722 |   const out = new Map();
  723 |   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
  724 | 
  725 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  726 |     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  727 |     out.set(g, n / denom);
  728 |   }
  729 | 
  730 |   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
  731 | }
  732 | 
  733 | /* =========================
  734 |    Recência / pressão estrutural
  735 | ========================= */
  736 | 
  737 | function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
  738 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  739 |   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
  740 |     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
  741 |     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
  742 |     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
  743 |     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
  744 |     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
  745 |     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
  746 |     return tsa - tsb;
  747 |   });
  748 | }
  749 | 
  750 | function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
  751 |   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
  752 |   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  753 |   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  754 |   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
  755 | 
  756 |   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
  757 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  758 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  759 |     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  760 |     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
  761 |   });
  762 | 
  763 |   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
  764 | }
  765 | 
  766 | function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  767 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  768 |   const out = new Map();
  769 | 
  770 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  771 |     out.set(g, {
  772 |       recentTop5: 0,
  773 |       recentIndirect: 0,
  774 |       recentFirst: 0,
  775 |       recentLast1First: 0,
  776 |       recentLast2Top5: 0,
  777 |     });
  778 |   }
  779 | 
  780 |   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
  781 |   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
  782 |   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
  783 | 
  784 |   for (const d of list) {
  785 |     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  786 | 
  787 |     for (const it of items) {
  788 |       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  789 |       const pos = Number(it.pos);
  790 |       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  791 | 
  792 |       const row = out.get(g);
  793 |       row.recentTop5 += 1;
  794 | 
  795 |       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
  796 |       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
  797 |     }
  798 |   }
  799 | 
  800 |   for (const d of last1) {
  801 |     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  802 |     for (const it of items) {
  803 |       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  804 |       const pos = Number(it.pos);
  805 |       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  806 |       if (pos === 1) {
  807 |         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
  808 |       }
  809 |     }

 2845 | function emptyGroupMap() {
 2846 |   const out = new Map();
 2847 |   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
 2848 |   return out;
 2849 | }
 2850 | 
 2851 | function getTop5GroupSet(draw) {
 2852 |   return new Set(
 2853 |     getAllTop5Groups(draw)
 2854 |       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
 2855 |       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
 2856 |   );
 2857 | }

 2859 | function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
 2860 |   const out = new Map();
 2861 |   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
 2862 |   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
 2863 |   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
 2864 |   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
 2865 | 
 2866 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2867 |     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
 2868 |     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
 2869 |   }
 2870 | 
 2871 |   return out;
 2872 | }
 2873 | 
 2874 | function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
 2875 |   const n = Number(samples || 0);
 2876 |   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
 2877 |   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
 2878 | }

 3243 | function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
 3244 |   const candidates = [
 3245 |     prize?.milhar,
 3246 |     prize?.milhar4,
 3247 |     prize?.numero,
 3248 |     prize?.number,
 3249 |     prize?.value,
 3250 |     prize?.result,
 3251 |     prize?.resultado,
 3252 |     prize?.premio,
 3253 |   ];
 3254 | 
 3255 |   for (const v of candidates) {
 3256 |     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
 3257 |     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
 3258 |   }
 3259 | 
 3260 |   return "";
 3261 | }
 3262 | 
 3263 | function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
 3264 |   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3265 |   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
 3266 | 
 3267 |   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
 3268 |   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3269 | 
 3270 |   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3271 |   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3272 | 
 3273 |   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
 3274 | }
 3275 | 
 3276 | function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
 3277 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 3278 | 
 3279 |   const rows = prizes
 3280 |     .map((p) => {
 3281 |       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
 3282 |       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
 3283 |       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
 3284 | 
 3285 |       return {
 3286 |         pos,
 3287 |         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
 3288 |         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
 3289 |         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
 3290 |         grupo,
 3291 |       };
 3292 |     })
 3293 |     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
 3294 |     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
 3295 | 
 3296 |   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
 3297 |   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
 3298 |   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
 3299 |   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
 3300 | 
 3301 |   const countMap = (arr) => {
 3302 |     const m = new Map();
 3303 |     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
 3304 |     return Array.from(m.entries())
 3305 |       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
 3306 |       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
 3307 |   };
 3308 | 
 3309 |   return {
 3310 |     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
 3311 |     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
 3312 |     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
 3313 |     grupos,
 3314 |     dezenas,
 3315 |     centenas,
 3316 |     milhares,
 3317 |     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
 3318 |     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
 3319 |     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
 3320 |     rows,
 3321 |     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
 3322 |   };
 3323 | }

 3325 | 
 3326 | 
 3327 | function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
 3328 |   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
 3329 | 
 3330 |   let score = 0;
 3331 |   const details = {};
 3332 | 
 3333 |   const add = (key, value) => {
 3334 |     if (!value) return;
 3335 |     score += value;
 3336 |     details[key] = value;
 3337 |   };
 3338 | 
 3339 |   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
 3340 |   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
 3341 | 
 3342 |   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
 3343 |   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
 3344 | 
 3345 |   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
 3346 |   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
 3347 | 
 3348 |   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
 3349 |     add("firstGrupo",45);
 3350 | 
 3351 |   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
 3352 |     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
 3353 |       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
 3354 |   }
 3355 | 
 3356 |   add(
 3357 |     "groupOverlap",
 3358 |     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
 3359 |   );
 3360 | 
 3361 |   add(
 3362 |     "dezenaOverlap",
 3363 |     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
 3364 |   );
 3365 | 
 3366 |   add(
 3367 |     "centenaOverlap",
 3368 |     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
 3369 |   );
 3370 | 
 3371 |   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3372 |   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3373 | 
 3374 |   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
 3375 |     add("repeatExists",8);
 3376 | 
 3377 |   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
 3378 |     add("repeatEqual",6);
 3379 | 
 3380 |   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
 3381 |     add("signature",60);
 3382 | 
 3383 |   if (a.hour === b.hour)
 3384 |     add("hour",8);
 3385 | 
 3386 |   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
 3387 | }

 3391 | 
 3392 | function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
 3393 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 3394 |   if (!currentScene) return [];
 3395 | 
 3396 |   const ordered = list
 3397 |     .map((draw) => ({
 3398 |       draw,
 3399 |       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
 3400 |     }))
 3401 |     .filter((x) => x.scene)
 3402 |     .sort((a, b) => {
 3403 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3404 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3405 |       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
 3406 |     });
 3407 | 
 3408 |   return ordered
 3409 |     .map((item, idx) => {
 3410 |       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
 3411 |       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
 3412 | 
 3413 |       return {
 3414 |         draw: item.draw,
 3415 |         scene: item.scene,
 3416 |         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
 3417 |         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
 3418 |         score,
 3419 |       };
 3420 |     })
 3421 |     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
 3422 |     .sort((a, b) => {
 3423 |       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
 3424 |         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3425 |       }
 3426 | 
 3427 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3428 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3429 |       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
 3430 |     })
 3431 |     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
 3432 | }

 3437 | function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
 3438 |   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
 3439 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 3440 | 
 3441 |   const freq = new Map();
 3442 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
 3443 | 
 3444 |   let totalWeight = 0;
 3445 | 
 3446 |   for (const item of ranking) {
 3447 |     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
 3448 |     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
 3449 | 
 3450 |     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
 3451 |     if (score <= 0) continue;
 3452 | 
 3453 |     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
 3454 |     totalWeight += score;
 3455 |   }
 3456 | 
 3457 |   const prob = new Map();
 3458 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 3459 |     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
 3460 |   }
 3461 | 
 3462 |   return {
 3463 |     freq,
 3464 |     prob,
 3465 |     samples: ranking.length,
 3466 |     totalWeight,
 3467 |   };
 3468 | }

 3871 |   function groupBy(keyFn) {
 3872 |     const map = new Map();
 3873 | 
 3874 |     for (const slot of validated) {

   32 | function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
   33 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   34 |   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
   35 |   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
   36 | 
   37 |   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
   38 | 
   39 |   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
   40 | 
   41 |   return list
   42 |     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
   43 |     .map((draw) => {
   44 |       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   45 |       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   46 |       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
   47 |     })
   48 |     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
   49 |     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
   50 | }

   56 | function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
   57 |   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
   58 |   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
   59 | 
   60 |   const n = Number(s);
   61 |   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
   62 |   if (n === 0) return 25;
   63 | 
   64 |   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
   65 |   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
   66 | }
   67 | 
   68 | function drawQualityScore(draw) {
   69 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   70 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   71 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   72 | 
   73 |   let score = 0;
   74 |   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
   75 |   if (h) score += 20;
   76 |   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
   77 | 
   78 |   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
   79 |   if (hasP1) score += 15;
   80 | 
   81 |   return score;
   82 | }

   84 | export function guessPrizePos(p) {
   85 |   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))

   87 |     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
   88 |       ? Number(p.posicao)
   89 |       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
   90 |         ? Number(p.pos)
   91 |         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
   92 |           ? Number(p.colocacao)
   93 |           : null;
   94 | 
   95 |   return pos;
   96 | }

  261 | export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
  262 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  263 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  264 | 
  265 |   const sorted = [...list]
  266 |     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  267 |     .sort((a, b) => {
  268 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  269 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  270 |       return tb - ta;
  271 |     });
  272 | 
  273 |   return sorted[0] || null;
  274 | }

  276 | export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
  277 |   draws,
  278 |   targetHourBucket,
  279 |   schedule
  280 | ) {
  281 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  282 |   if (!list.length) return null;
  283 | 
  284 |   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
  285 |   if (t < 0) return null;
  286 | 
  287 |   const candidates = list
  288 |     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  289 |     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
  290 |     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
  291 |     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
  292 | 
  293 |   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
  294 | }

  548 | export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
  549 |   const map = new Map();
  550 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  551 | 
  552 |   for (const d of list) {
  553 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  554 |     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
  555 |     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
  556 | 
  557 |     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  558 | 
  559 |     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
  560 |     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
  561 | 
  562 |     if (!prev) {
  563 |       map.set(drawKey, d);
  564 |       continue;
  565 |     }
  566 | 
  567 |     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
  568 |       map.set(drawKey, d);
  569 |     }
  570 |   }
  571 | 
  572 |   return map;

  670 | function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  671 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  672 |   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
  673 |   const out = new Map();
  674 | 
  675 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  676 |     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  677 |     out.set(g, n / denom);
  678 |   }
  679 | 
  680 |   return out;
  681 | }

  683 | function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
  684 |   draws,
  685 |   lotteryKey,
  686 |   targetHour,
  687 |   targetDow,
  688 |   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  689 | ) {
  690 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  691 |   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  692 |   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  693 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  694 | 
  695 |   const freq = new Map();
  696 |   let totalSamples = 0;
  697 | 
  698 |   for (const d of list) {
  699 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  700 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  701 |     if (!h || !y) continue;
  702 | 
  703 |     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  704 |       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
  705 |       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
  706 |     } else {
  707 |       if (h !== target) continue;
  708 |     }
  709 | 
  710 |     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  711 |     if (!items.length) continue;
  712 | 
  713 |     totalSamples += 1;
  714 | 
  715 |     for (const it of items) {
  716 |       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  717 |       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  718 |       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  719 |     }
  720 |   }
  721 | 
  722 |   const out = new Map();
  723 |   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
  724 | 
  725 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  726 |     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  727 |     out.set(g, n / denom);
  728 |   }
  729 | 
  730 |   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
  731 | }

  736 | 
  737 | function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
  738 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  739 |   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
  740 |     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
  741 |     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
  742 |     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
  743 |     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
  744 |     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
  745 |     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
  746 |     return tsa - tsb;
  747 |   });
  748 | }
  749 | 
  750 | function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
  751 |   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
  752 |   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  753 |   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  754 |   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
  755 | 
  756 |   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
  757 |     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  758 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  759 |     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  760 |     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
  761 |   });
  762 | 
  763 |   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
  764 | }

 2799 | function emptyGroupMap() {
 2800 |   const out = new Map();
 2801 |   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
 2802 |   return out;
 2803 | }
 2804 | 
 2805 | function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
 2806 |   return new Set(
 2807 |     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
 2808 |       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
 2809 |       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
 2810 |   );
 2811 | }
 2812 | 
 2813 | function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
 2814 |   const out = new Map();
 2815 |   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
 2816 |   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
 2817 |   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
 2818 |   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
 2819 | 
 2820 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2821 |     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
 2822 |     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
 2823 |   }
 2824 | 
 2825 |   return out;
 2826 | }
 2827 | 
 2828 | function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
 2829 |   const n = Number(samples || 0);
 2830 |   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
 2831 |   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
 2832 | }

 3197 | function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
 3198 |   const candidates = [
 3199 |     prize?.milhar,
 3200 |     prize?.milhar4,
 3201 |     prize?.numero,
 3202 |     prize?.number,
 3203 |     prize?.value,
 3204 |     prize?.result,
 3205 |     prize?.resultado,
 3206 |     prize?.premio,
 3207 |   ];
 3208 | 
 3209 |   for (const v of candidates) {
 3210 |     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
 3211 |     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
 3212 |   }
 3213 | 
 3214 |   return "";
 3215 | }
 3216 | 
 3217 | function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
 3218 |   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3219 |   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
 3220 | 
 3221 |   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
 3222 |   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3223 | 
 3224 |   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3225 |   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3226 | 
 3227 |   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
 3228 | }

 3230 | function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
 3231 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 3232 | 
 3233 |   const rows = prizes
 3234 |     .map((p) => {
 3235 |       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
 3236 |       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
 3237 |       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
 3238 | 
 3239 |       return {
 3240 |         pos,
 3241 |         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
 3242 |         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
 3243 |         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
 3244 |         grupo,
 3245 |       };
 3246 |     })
 3247 |     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
 3248 |     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
 3249 | 
 3250 |   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
 3251 |   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
 3252 |   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
 3253 |   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
 3254 | 
 3255 |   const countMap = (arr) => {
 3256 |     const m = new Map();
 3257 |     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
 3258 |     return Array.from(m.entries())
 3259 |       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
 3260 |       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
 3261 |   };
 3262 | 
 3263 |   return {
 3264 |     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
 3265 |     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
 3266 |     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
 3267 |     grupos,
 3268 |     dezenas,
 3269 |     centenas,
 3270 |     milhares,
 3271 |     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
 3272 |     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
 3273 |     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
 3274 |     rows,
 3275 |     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
 3276 |   };
 3277 | }

 3281 | function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
 3282 |   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
 3283 | 
 3284 |   let score = 0;
 3285 |   const details = {};
 3286 | 
 3287 |   const add = (key, value) => {
 3288 |     if (!value) return;
 3289 |     score += value;
 3290 |     details[key] = value;
 3291 |   };
 3292 | 
 3293 |   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
 3294 |   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
 3295 | 
 3296 |   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
 3297 |   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
 3298 | 
 3299 |   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
 3300 |   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
 3301 | 
 3302 |   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
 3303 |     add("firstGrupo",45);
 3304 | 
 3305 |   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
 3306 |     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
 3307 |       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
 3308 |   }
 3309 | 
 3310 |   add(
 3311 |     "groupOverlap",
 3312 |     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
 3313 |   );
 3314 | 
 3315 |   add(
 3316 |     "dezenaOverlap",
 3317 |     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
 3318 |   );
 3319 | 
 3320 |   add(
 3321 |     "centenaOverlap",
 3322 |     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
 3323 |   );
 3324 | 
 3325 |   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3326 |   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3327 | 
 3328 |   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
 3329 |     add("repeatExists",8);
 3330 | 
 3331 |   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
 3332 |     add("repeatEqual",6);
 3333 | 
 3334 |   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
 3335 |     add("signature",60);
 3336 | 
 3337 |   if (a.hour === b.hour)
 3338 |     add("hour",8);
 3339 | 
 3340 |   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
 3341 | }

 3346 | function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
 3347 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 3348 |   if (!currentScene) return [];
 3349 | 
 3350 |   const ordered = list
 3351 |     .map((draw) => ({
 3352 |       draw,
 3353 |       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
 3354 |     }))
 3355 |     .filter((x) => x.scene)
 3356 |     .sort((a, b) => {
 3357 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3358 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3359 |       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
 3360 |     });
 3361 | 
 3362 |   return ordered
 3363 |     .map((item, idx) => {
 3364 |       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
 3365 |       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
 3366 | 
 3367 |       return {
 3368 |         draw: item.draw,
 3369 |         scene: item.scene,
 3370 |         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
 3371 |         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
 3372 |         score,
 3373 |       };
 3374 |     })
 3375 |     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
 3376 |     .sort((a, b) => {
 3377 |       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
 3378 |         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3379 |       }
 3380 | 
 3381 |       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3382 |       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3383 |       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
 3384 |     })
 3385 |     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
 3386 | }

 3390 | 
 3391 | function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
 3392 |   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
 3393 |   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 3394 | 
 3395 |   const freq = new Map();
 3396 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
 3397 | 
 3398 |   let totalWeight = 0;
 3399 | 
 3400 |   for (const item of ranking) {
 3401 |     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
 3402 |     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
 3403 | 
 3404 |     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
 3405 |     if (score <= 0) continue;
 3406 | 
 3407 |     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
 3408 |     totalWeight += score;
 3409 |   }
 3410 | 
 3411 |   const prob = new Map();
 3412 |   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 3413 |     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
 3414 |   }
 3415 | 
 3416 |   return {
 3417 |     freq,
 3418 |     prob,
 3419 |     samples: ranking.length,
 3420 |     totalWeight,
 3421 |   };
 3422 | }

 3426 |    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
 3427 | ========================= */
 3428 | 
 3429 | function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
 3430 |   const g = Number(grupo2);
 3431 |   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
 3432 | 
 3433 |   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
 3434 |   const out = [];
 3435 | 
 3436 |   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
 3437 |     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
 3438 |   }
 3439 | 
 3440 |   return out.filter(Boolean);
 3441 | }

 3798 |   function pct(n, d) {
 3799 |     if (!d) return 0;
 3800 |     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
 3801 |   }
 3802 | 
 3803 |   function groupBy(keyFn) {
 3804 |     const map = new Map();
 3805 | 
 3806 |     for (const slot of validated) {
 3807 |       const key = keyFn(slot);
 3808 |       const current = map.get(key) || {
 3809 |         key,
 3810 |         total: 0,
 3811 |         top1Hits: 0,
 3812 |         top3Hits: 0,
 3813 |       };
 3814 | 
 3815 |       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
 3816 |       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
 3817 |       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
 3818 | 
 3819 |       current.total += 1;
 3820 |       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
 3821 |       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
 3822 | 
 3823 |       map.set(key, current);
 3824 |     }
 3825 | 
 3826 |     return Array.from(map.values())
 3827 |       .map((x) => ({
 3828 |         ...x,
 3829 |         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
 3830 |         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
 3831 |       }))
 3832 |       .sort((a, b) => {
 3833 |         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
 3834 |         return b.total - a.total;
 3835 |       });
 3836 |   }
 3837 | 
 3838 |   return {
 3839 |     lotteryKey,
 3840 |     total,
 3841 |     top1Hits,
 3842 |     top3Hits,
 3843 |     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
 3844 |     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
 3845 |     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
 3846 |     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
 3847 |     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
 3848 |       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
 3849 |       hour: slot.targetHour,
 3850 |       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
 3851 |       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
 3852 |       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
 3853 |       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3854 |         .slice(0, 3)
 3855 |         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
 3856 |       top1Hit:
 3857 |         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
 3858 |       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3859 |         .slice(0, 3)
 3860 |         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
 3861 |       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,
 3862 |     })),

   81 | function emptyAnalytics() {

   85 | function drawTs(draw) {
   86 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   87 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   88 | 
   89 |   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
   90 | 
   91 |   const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
   92 |   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   93 | 
   94 |   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
   95 |     return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
   96 |   }
   97 | 
   98 |   return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
   99 | }
  100 | 
  101 | function drawKey(draw) {
  102 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
  103 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
  104 |   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
  105 | }
  106 | 
  107 | function hasDrawAtHour(draws, hourBucket) {
  108 |   const target = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
  109 |   if (!target) return false;
  110 | 
  111 |   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some((d) => {
  112 |     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  113 |     return h === target;
  114 |   });
  115 | }
  116 | 
  117 | function mergeBaseIntoRange(rangeDraws, baseDraw) {
  118 |   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
  119 |   const key = drawKey(baseDraw);
  120 | 
  121 |   if (!key) return list;
  122 | 
  123 |   const map = new Map();
  124 | 
  125 |   for (const d of list) {
  126 |     const k = drawKey(d);
  127 |     if (k) map.set(k, d);
  128 |   }
  129 | 
  130 |   if (!map.has(key)) {
  131 |     map.set(key, baseDraw);
  132 |   }
  133 | 
  134 |   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
  135 | }
  136 | 
  137 | function isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(draw, lotteryKey) {
  138 |   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
  139 |   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
  140 | 
  141 |   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
  142 | 
  143 |   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
  144 |     lotteryKey,
  145 |     ymd: y,
  146 |     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  147 |     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  148 |     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  149 |   });
  150 | 
  151 |   const normalizedSchedule = Array.isArray(schedule)
  152 |     ? schedule.map(toHourBucket).filter(Boolean)
  153 |     : [];
  154 | 
  155 |   return normalizedSchedule.includes(h);
  156 | }

  158 | function findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
  159 |   draws,
  160 |   lotteryKey,
  161 |   targetYmd,
  162 |   targetHourBucket,
  163 | }) {
  164 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  165 |   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
  166 | 
  167 |   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) {
  168 |     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
  169 |   }
  170 | 
  171 |   const [Y, M, D] = String(targetYmd).split("-").map(Number);
  172 |   const mins = hourToInt(targetHour);
  173 |   const targetTs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
  174 | 
  175 |   let best = null;
  176 |   let bestTs = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
  177 | 
  178 |   for (const d of list) {
  179 |     if (!isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey)) continue;
  180 | 
  181 |     const ts = drawTs(d);
  182 |     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
  183 |     if (ts >= targetTs) continue;
  184 | 
  185 |     if (ts > bestTs) {
  186 |       best = d;
  187 |       bestTs = ts;
  188 |     }
  189 |   }
  190 | 
  191 |   if (!best) {
  192 |     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
  193 |   }
  194 | 
  195 |   return {
  196 |     draw: best,
  197 |     ymd: pickDrawYMD(best) || "",
  198 |     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(best)) || "",
  199 |     source: "history_range",
  200 |   };
  201 | }
  202 | 
  203 | function sanitizeHistoricalDraws({ draws, lotteryKey, baseDraw }) {
  204 |   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  205 |   const baseTs = drawTs(baseDraw);
  206 | 
  207 |   if (!baseDraw || !Number.isFinite(baseTs)) return [];
  208 | 
  209 |   return list
  210 |     .filter((d) => isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey))
  211 |     .filter((d) => {
  212 |       const ts = drawTs(d);
  213 |       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  214 |     })
  215 |     .sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
  216 | }
  217 | 
  218 | function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
  219 |   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
  220 | 
  221 |   const h = toHourBucket(hour);
  222 |   const m = String(h || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
  223 | 
  224 |   if (!m) return null;
  225 | 
  226 |   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
  227 |   const hh = Number(m[1]);
  228 | 
  229 |   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
  230 |   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
  231 | }
  232 | 
  233 | function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
  234 |   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
  235 |   if (!target) return false;
  236 |   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
  237 | }

  243 | }

  255 |     .map((v) => safeStr(v))
  256 |     .filter(Boolean);

  265 |     new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(
  266 |       "debugTop3Effects"

  273 |     (window.__TOP3_EFFECT_COUNTS__ = {});
  274 | 
  275 |   counters[name] = Number(counters[name] || 0) + 1;
  276 | 
  277 |   console.info(
  278 |     "[TOP3 EFFECT]",
  279 |     name,
  280 |     `#${counters[name]}`,
  281 |     details
  282 |   );

  109 | function scoreLogEntry(entry) {
  110 |   if (!entry) return -1;
  111 | 
  112 |   let score = 0;
  113 |   if (Array.isArray(entry?.picks) && entry.picks.length) score += 20;
  114 |   if (entry?.result != null) score += 10;
  115 |   if (typeof entry?.hit === "boolean") score += 5;
  116 |   if (entry?.validatedAt) score += 2;
  117 |   if (entry?.createdAt) score += 1;
  118 | 
  119 |   return score;
  120 | }
  121 | 
  122 | function normalizeAndDedupeLog(log) {
  123 |   const list = Array.isArray(log) ? log : [];
  124 |   const byKey = new Map();
  125 | 
  126 |   for (const raw of list) {
  127 |     const entry = normalizeLogEntry(raw);
  128 |     if (!entry) continue;
  129 | 
  130 |     const prev = byKey.get(entry.targetKey);
  131 | 
  132 |     if (!prev || scoreLogEntry(entry) >= scoreLogEntry(prev)) {
  133 |       byKey.set(entry.targetKey, entry);
  134 |     }
  135 |   }
  136 | 
  137 |   return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => {
  138 |     const aKey = `${a?.target?.ymd || ""}_${a?.target?.hour || ""}`;
  139 |     const bKey = `${b?.target?.ymd || ""}_${b?.target?.hour || ""}`;
  140 |     return aKey < bKey ? -1 : aKey > bKey ? 1 : 0;
  141 |   });
  142 | }
  143 | 
  144 | function getTop3Log() {
  145 |   try {
  146 |     const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("top3_log") || "[]");
  147 |     return normalizeAndDedupeLog(raw);
  148 |   } catch {
  149 |     return [];
  150 |   }
  151 | }
  152 | 
  153 | function saveTop3Log(log) {
  154 |   try {
  155 |     const normalized = normalizeAndDedupeLog(log).slice(-200);
  156 |     localStorage.setItem("top3_log", JSON.stringify(normalized));
  157 |   } catch {}
  158 | }

  161 |   targetKey,

  205 |       createdAt: prev.createdAt || Date.now(),

    7 | export function normalizePositions(positions) {
    8 |   const arr =
    9 |     Array.isArray(positions) && positions.length
   10 |       ? positions
   11 |           .map(Number)
   12 |           .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)
   13 |       : null;
   14 | 
   15 |   if (!arr || !arr.length) return null;
   16 |   return Array.from(new Set(arr)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
   17 | }
   18 | 
   19 | export function isValidGrupo(n) {
   20 |   return Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 1 && Number(n) <= 25;
   21 | }
   22 | 
   23 | export function isValidPosition(n) {
   24 |   return Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 1 && Number(n) <= 10;
   25 | }
   26 | 
   27 | export function extractIntInRange(value, min, max) {
   28 |   if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
   29 | 
   30 |   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
   31 |     const n = Math.trunc(value);
   32 |     return n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   33 |   }
   34 | 
   35 |   const s = String(value).trim();
   36 |   if (!s) return null;
   37 | 
   38 |   if (/^\d+$/.test(s)) {
   39 |     const n = Number(s);
   40 |     return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   41 |   }
   42 | 
   43 |   const m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
   44 |   if (!m) return null;
   45 |   const n = Number(m[1]);
   46 |   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   47 | }
   48 | 
   49 | export function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   50 |   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   51 |   if (!s) return "";
   52 |   return s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
   53 | }
   54 | 
   55 | export function prizeWidthByPosition(position) {
   56 |   return Number(position) === 7 ? 3 : 4;
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | export function toPrizeDigitsByPosition(input, position) {
   60 |   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(input);
   61 |   if (!digits) return null;
   62 | 
   63 |   const w = prizeWidthByPosition(position);
   64 | 
   65 |   if (w === 3) {
   66 |     const last3 = digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
   67 |     return /^\d{3}$/.test(last3) ? last3 : null;
   68 |   }
   69 | 
   70 |   const last4 = digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
   71 |   return /^\d{4}$/.test(last4) ? last4 : null;
   72 | }
   73 | 
   74 | export function digitsToDezena2(numStr) {
   75 |   const s = String(numStr || "");
   76 |   if (!/^\d{2,4}$/.test(s)) return null;
   77 |   return s.slice(-2);
   78 | }
   79 | 
   80 | export function digitsToCentena3(numStr) {
   81 |   const s = String(numStr || "");
   82 |   if (!/^\d{3,4}$/.test(s)) return null;
   83 |   return s.slice(-3);
   84 | }

 1004 | function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
 1005 |   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 1006 |   if (!prizes.length) return "";
 1007 | 
 1008 |   const parts = prizes
 1009 |     .map((p) => {
 1010 |       const pos = Number(p?.position);
 1011 |       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
 1012 |       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
 1013 |         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
 1014 |         pos
 1015 |       );
 1016 | 
 1017 |       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
 1018 |       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
 1019 |     })
 1020 |     .filter(Boolean)
 1021 |     .sort();
 1022 | 
 1023 |   return parts.join("|");
 1024 | }

 1056 |     ? b.prizes.length
 1057 |     : 0;

  601 |   // ✅ percentuais estáveis: fecha em 100 sem ruído de arredondamento
  602 |   const rawSum =
  603 |     best.reduce((acc, x) => acc + (Number(x.finalScore) || 0), 0) || 1;
  604 | 
  605 |   const rawPcts = best.map((x) => (((Number(x.finalScore) || 0) / rawSum) * 100));
  606 |   const floored = rawPcts.map((x) => Math.floor(x));
  607 |   let remainder = 100 - floored.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  608 | 
  609 |   const order = rawPcts
  610 |     .map((x, i) => ({ i, frac: x - Math.floor(x) }))
  611 |     .sort((a, b) => b.frac - a.frac);
  612 | 
  613 |   const pctFinal = [...floored];
  614 |   for (let i = 0; i < order.length && remainder > 0; i += 1) {
  615 |     pctFinal[order[i].i] += 1;
  616 |     remainder -= 1;
  617 |   }
  618 | 
  619 |   const top = best.map((x, idx) => {
  620 |     return {
  621 |       ...x,
  622 |       rank: idx + 1,
  623 |       pct: pctFinal[idx] ?? 0,
  624 |       title:
  625 |         idx === 0 ? "Principal" : idx === 1 ? "Alternativa" : "Terceira opção",
  626 |     };
  627 |   });

  629 |   return {
